ne24:2.2	“Tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagocaro”ti idaṁ satindriyaṁ, satindriye gahite gahitāni bhavanti pañcindriyāni.
ne24:2.3	“Sammādiṭṭhipurekkhāro”ti sammādiṭṭhiyā gahitāya gahito bhavati ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo.
ne24:2.4	Taṁ kissa hetu?
ne24:2.5	Sammādiṭṭhito hi sammāsaṅkappo pabhavati, sammāsaṅkappato sammāvācā pabhavati, sammāvācāto sammākammanto pabhavati, sammākammantato sammāājīvo pabhavati, sammāājīvato sammāvāyāmo pabhavati, sammāvāyāmato sammāsati pabhavati, sammāsatito sammāsamādhi pabhavati, sammāsamādhito sammāvimutti pabhavati, sammāvimuttito sammāvimuttiñāṇadassanaṁ pabhavati.
ne24:2.6	Niyutto lakkhaṇo hārasampāto.
ne25:0.1	Netti
ne25:0.2	Paṭiniddesavāra
ne25:0.3	Sampāta 6
ne25:0.4	Catubyūhahārasampāta
ne25:1.1	Tattha katamo catubyūho hārasampāto.
ne25:2.1	“Tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagocaro”ti gāthā.
ne25:2.2	“Tasmā rakkhitacittassā”ti rakkhitaṁ paripālīyatīti esā nirutti.
ne25:2.3	Idha bhagavato ko adhippāyo?
ne25:2.4	Ye duggatīhi parimuccitukāmā bhavissanti, te dhammacārino bhavissantīti ayaṁ ettha bhagavato adhippāyo.
ne25:2.5	Kokāliko hi sāriputtamoggallānesu theresu cittaṁ padosayitvā mahāpadumaniraye upapanno.
ne25:2.6	Bhagavā ca satiārakkhena cetasā samannāgato, suttamhi vuttaṁ—
ne25:2.7	“satiyā cittaṁ rakkhitabban”ti.
ne25:2.8	Niyutto catubyūho hārasampāto.
ne26:0.1	Netti
ne26:0.2	Paṭiniddesavāra
ne26:0.3	Sampāta 7
ne26:0.4	Āvaṭṭahārasampāta
ne26:1.1	Tattha katamo āvaṭṭo hārasampāto?
ne26:2.1	“Tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagocaro”ti gāthā.
ne26:2.2	“Tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagocaro”ti samatho.
ne26:2.3	“Sammādiṭṭhipurekkhāro”ti vipassanā.
ne26:2.4	“Ñatvāna udayabbayan”ti dukkhapariññā.
ne26:2.5	“Thinamiddhābhibhū bhikkhū”ti samudayapahānaṁ.
ne26:2.6	“Sabbā duggatiyo jahe”ti nirodho.
ne26:2.7	Imāni cattāri saccāni.
ne26:2.8	Niyutto āvaṭṭo hārasampāto.
ne27:0.1	Netti
ne27:0.2	Paṭiniddesavāra
ne27:0.3	Sampāta 8
ne27:0.4	Vibhattihārasampāta
ne27:1.1	Tattha katamo vibhattihārasampāto?
ne27:2.1	“Tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagocaro”ti gāthā.
ne27:2.2	Kusalapakkho kusalapakkhena niddisitabbo.
ne27:2.3	Akusalapakkho akusalapakkhena niddisitabbo.
ne27:2.4	Niyutto vibhattihārasampāto.
ne28:0.1	Netti
ne28:0.2	Paṭiniddesavāra
ne28:0.3	Sampāta 9
ne28:0.4	Parivattanahārasampāta
ne28:1.1	Tattha katamo parivattano hārasampāto?
ne28:2.1	“Tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagocaro”ti gāthā.
ne28:2.2	Samathavipassanāya bhāvitāya nirodho phalaṁ, pariññātaṁ dukkhaṁ, samudayo pahīno, maggo bhāvito paṭipakkhena.
ne28:2.3	Niyutto parivattano hārasampāto.
ne29:0.1	Netti
ne29:0.2	Paṭiniddesavāra
ne29:0.3	Sampāta 10
ne29:0.4	Vevacanahārasampāta
ne29:1.1	Tattha katamo vevacano hārasampāto?
ne29:2.1	“Tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagocaro”ti gāthā.
ne29:2.2	“Tasmā rakkhitacittassā”ti cittaṁ mano viññāṇaṁ manindriyaṁ manāyatanaṁ vijānanā vijānitattaṁ, idaṁ vevacanaṁ.
ne29:2.3	“Sammāsaṅkappagocaro”ti nekkhammasaṅkappo abyāpādasaṅkappo avihiṁsāsaṅkappo, idaṁ vevacanaṁ.
ne29:2.4	“Sammādiṭṭhipurekkhāro”ti sammādiṭṭhi nāma paññāsatthaṁ paññākhaggo paññāratanaṁ paññāpajjoto paññāpatodo paññāpāsādo, idaṁ vevacanaṁ.
ne29:2.5	Niyutto vevacano hārasampāto.
ne30:0.1	Netti
ne30:0.2	Paṭiniddesavāra
ne30:0.3	Sampāta 11
ne30:0.4	Paññattihārasampāta
ne30:1.1	Tattha katamo paññattihārasampāto?
ne30:2.1	“Tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagocaro”ti gāthā.
ne30:2.2	“Tasmā rakkhitacittassā”ti padaṭṭhānapaññatti satiyā.
ne30:2.3	“Sammāsaṅkappagocaro”ti bhāvanāpaññatti samathassa.
ne30:2.4	“Sammādiṭṭhipurekkhāro, ñatvāna udayabbayan”ti dassanabhūmiyā nikkhepapaññatti.
ne30:2.5	“Thinamiddhābhibhū bhikkhū”ti samudayassa anavasesappahānapaññatti, “sabbā duggatiyo jahe”ti bhāvanāpaññatti maggassa.
ne30:2.6	Niyutto paññattihārasampāto.
ne31:0.1	Netti
ne31:0.2	Paṭiniddesavāra
ne31:0.3	Sampāta 12
ne31:0.4	Otaraṇahārasampāta
ne31:1.1	Tattha katamo otaraṇo hārasampāto?
ne31:2.1	“Tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagocaro”ti gāthā.
ne31:2.2	“Tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagocaro”.
ne31:2.3	“Sammādiṭṭhipurekkhāro”ti sammādiṭṭhiyā gahitāya gahitāni bhavanti pañcindriyāni, ayaṁ indriyehi otaraṇā.
ne31:3.1	Tāniyeva indriyāni vijjā, vijjuppādā avijjānirodho, avijjānirodhā saṅkhāranirodho, saṅkhāranirodhā viññāṇanirodho, evaṁ sabbaṁ, ayaṁ paṭiccasamuppādena otaraṇā.
ne31:4.1	Tāniyeva pañcindriyāni tīhi khandhehi saṅgahitāni—
ne31:4.2	sīlakkhandhena samādhikkhandhena paññākkhandhena.
ne31:4.3	Ayaṁ khandhehi otaraṇā.
ne31:5.1	Tāni yeva pañcindriyāni saṅkhārapariyāpannāni.
ne31:5.2	Ye saṅkhārā anāsavā no ca bhavaṅgā, te saṅkhārā dhammadhātusaṅgahitā, ayaṁ dhātūhi otaraṇā.
ne31:6.1	Sā dhammadhātu dhammāyatanapariyāpannā, yaṁ āyatanaṁ anāsavaṁ no ca bhavaṅgaṁ, ayaṁ āyatanehi otaraṇā.
ne31:6.2	Niyutto otaraṇo hārasampāto.
ne32:0.1	Netti
ne32:0.2	Paṭiniddesavāra
ne32:0.3	Sampāta 13
ne32:0.4	Sodhanahārasampāta
ne32:1.1	Tattha katamo sodhano hārasampāto?
ne32:2.1	“Tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagocaro”ti gāthā.
ne32:2.2	Yattha ārambho suddho, so pañho visajjito bhavati.
ne32:2.3	Yattha pana ārambho na suddho, na tāva so pañho visajjito bhavati.
ne32:2.4	Niyutto sodhano hārasampāto.
ne33:0.1	Netti
ne33:0.2	Paṭiniddesavāra
ne33:0.3	Sampāta 14
ne33:0.4	Adhiṭṭhānahārasampāta
ne33:1.1	Tattha katamo adhiṭṭhāno hārasampāto?
ne33:2.1	“Tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagocaro”ti gāthā.
ne33:2.2	Tasmā rakkhitacittassāti ekattatā.
ne33:2.3	Cittaṁ mano viññāṇaṁ, ayaṁ vemattatā.
ne33:2.4	Sammāsaṅkappagocaroti ekattatā.
ne33:2.5	Nekkhammasaṅkappo abyāpādasaṅkappo avihiṁsāsaṅkappo ayaṁ vemattatā.
ne33:2.6	Sammādiṭṭhipurekkhāroti ekattatā.
ne33:2.7	Sammādiṭṭhi nāma yaṁ dukkhe ñāṇaṁ dukkhasamudaye ñāṇaṁ dukkhanirodhe ñāṇaṁ dukkhanirodhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṁ magge ñāṇaṁ hetumhi ñāṇaṁ hetusamuppannesu dhammesu ñāṇaṁ paccaye ñāṇaṁ paccayasamuppannesu dhammesu ñāṇaṁ, yaṁ tattha tattha yathābhūtaṁ ñāṇadassanaṁ abhisamayo sampaṭivedho saccāgamanaṁ, ayaṁ vemattatā.
ne33:2.8	Ñatvāna udayabbayanti ekattatā, udayena avijjāpaccayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṁ, evaṁ sabbaṁ samudayo bhavati.
ne33:2.9	Vayena avijjānirodhā saṅkhāranirodho, evaṁ sabbaṁ nirodho hoti, ayaṁ vemattatā.
ne33:2.10	Thinamiddhābhibhū bhikkhūti ekattatā, thinaṁ nāma yā cittassa akallatā akammaniyatā, middhaṁ nāma yaṁ kāyassa līnattaṁ, ayaṁ vemattatā.
ne33:2.11	Sabbā duggatiyo jaheti ekattatā, devamanusse vā upanidhāya apāyā duggati, nibbānaṁ vā upanidhāya sabbā upapattiyo duggati, ayaṁ vemattatā.
ne33:2.12	Niyutto adhiṭṭhāno hārasampāto.
ne34:0.1	Netti
ne34:0.2	Paṭiniddesavāra
ne34:0.3	Sampāta 15
ne34:0.4	Parikkhārahārasampāta
ne34:1.1	Tattha katamo parikkhāro hārasampāto?
ne34:2.1	“Tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagocaro”ti gāthā.
ne34:2.2	Ayaṁ samathavipassanāya parikkhāro.
ne34:2.3	Niyutto parikkhāro hārasampāto.
ne35:0.1	Netti
ne35:0.2	Paṭiniddesavāra
ne35:0.3	Sampāta 16
ne35:0.4	Samāropanahārasampāta
ne35:1.1	Tattha katamo samāropano hārasampāto?
ne35:2.1	“Tasmā rakkhitacittassa,
ne35:2.2	sammāsaṅkappagocaro;
ne35:2.3	Sammādiṭṭhipurekkhāro,
ne35:2.4	ñatvāna udayabbayaṁ;
ne35:2.5	Thinamiddhābhibhū bhikkhu,
ne35:2.6	sabbā duggatiyo jahe”ti.
ne35:3.1	“Tasmā rakkhitacittassā”ti tiṇṇaṁ sucaritānaṁ padaṭṭhānaṁ, citte rakkhite taṁ rakkhitaṁ bhavati kāyakammaṁ vacīkammaṁ manokammaṁ.
ne35:3.2	Sammādiṭṭhipurekkhāroti sammādiṭṭhiyā bhāvitāya bhāvito bhavati ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo.
ne35:3.3	Kena kāraṇena?
ne35:3.4	Sammādiṭṭhito hi sammāsaṅkappo pabhavati, sammāsaṅkappato sammāvācā pabhavati, sammāvācāto sammākammanto pabhavati, sammākammantato sammāājīvo pabhavati, sammāājīvato sammāvāyāmo pabhavati, sammāvāyāmato sammāsati pabhavati, sammāsatito sammāsamādhi pabhavati, sammāsamādhito sammāvimutti pabhavati, sammāvimuttito sammāvimuttiñāṇadassanaṁ pabhavati.
ne35:3.5	Ayaṁ anupādiseso puggalo anupādisesā ca nibbānadhātu.
ne35:3.6	Niyutto samāropano hārasampāto.
ne35:4.1	Tenāha āyasmā mahākaccāyano—
ne35:5.1	“Soḷasa hārā paṭhamaṁ,
ne35:5.2	Disalocanato disā viloketvā;
ne35:5.3	Saṅkhipiya aṅkusena hi,
ne35:5.4	Nayehi tīhi niddise suttan”ti.
ne35:5.5	Niyutto hārasampāto.
ne36:0.1	Netti
ne36:0.2	5. Nayasamuṭṭhāna
ne36:1.1	Tattha katamaṁ nayasamuṭṭhānaṁ?
ne36:1.2	Pubbā koṭi na paññāyati avijjāya ca bhavataṇhāya ca, tattha avijjānīvaraṇaṁ taṇhāsaṁyojanaṁ.
ne36:1.3	Avijjānīvaraṇā sattā avijjāsaṁyuttā avijjāpakkhena vicaranti, te vuccanti diṭṭhicaritāti.
ne36:1.4	Taṇhāsaṁyojanā sattā taṇhāsaṁyuttā taṇhāpakkhena vicaranti, te vuccanti taṇhācaritāti.
ne36:1.5	Diṭṭhicaritā ito bahiddhā pabbajitā attakilamathānuyogamanuyuttā viharanti.
ne36:1.6	Taṇhācaritā ito bahiddhā pabbajitā kāmesu kāmasukhallikānuyogamanuyuttā viharanti.
ne36:2.1	Tattha kiṅkāraṇaṁ yaṁ diṭṭhicaritā ito bahiddhā pabbajitā attakilamathānuyogamanuyuttā viharanti, taṇhācaritā ito bahiddhā pabbajitā kāmesu kāmasukhallikānuyogamanuyuttā viharanti?
ne36:2.2	Ito bahiddhā natthi saccavavatthānaṁ, kuto catusaccappakāsanā vā samathavipassanākosallaṁ vā upasamasukhappatti vā.
ne36:2.3	Te upasamasukhassa anabhiññā viparītacetā evamāhaṁsu “natthi sukhena sukhaṁ, dukkhena nāma sukhaṁ adhigantabban”ti.
ne36:2.4	Yo kāme paṭisevati, so lokaṁ vaḍḍhayati, yo lokaṁ vaḍḍhayati, so bahuṁ puññaṁ pasavatīti te evaṁsaññī evaṁdiṭṭhī dukkhena sukhaṁ patthayamānā kāmesu puññasaññī attakilamathānuyogamanuyuttā ca viharanti kāmasukhallikānuyogamanuyuttā ca, te tadabhiññā santā rogameva vaḍḍhayanti, gaṇḍameva vaḍḍhayanti, sallameva vaḍḍhayanti, te rogābhitunnā gaṇḍapaṭipīḷitā sallānuviddhā nirayatiracchānayonipetāsuresu ummujjanimujjāni karontā ugghātanigghātaṁ paccanubhontā rogagaṇḍasallabhesajjaṁ na vindanti.
ne36:2.5	Tattha attakilamathānuyogo kāmasukhallikānuyogo ca saṅkileso, samathavipassanā vodānaṁ.
ne36:2.6	Attakilamathānuyogo kāmasukhallikānuyogo ca rogo, samathavipassanā roganigghātakabhesajjaṁ.
ne36:2.7	Attakilamathānuyogo kāmasukhallikānuyogo ca gaṇḍo, samathavipassanā gaṇḍanigghātakabhesajjaṁ.
ne36:2.8	Attakilamathānuyogo kāmasukhallikānuyogo ca sallo, samathavipassanā salluddharaṇabhesajjaṁ.
ne36:3.1	Tattha saṅkileso dukkhaṁ, tadabhisaṅgo taṇhā samudayo, taṇhānirodho dukkhanirodho, samathavipassanā dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā, imāni cattāri saccāni.
ne36:3.2	Dukkhaṁ pariññeyyaṁ, samudayo pahātabbo, maggo bhāvetabbo, nirodho sacchikātabbo.
ne36:4.1	Tattha diṭṭhicaritā rūpaṁ attato upagacchanti.
ne36:4.2	Vedanaṁ …pe…
ne36:4.3	saññaṁ …
ne36:4.4	saṅkhāre …
ne36:4.5	viññāṇaṁ attato upagacchanti.
ne36:4.6	Taṇhācaritā rūpavantaṁ attānaṁ upagacchanti.
ne36:4.7	Attani vā rūpaṁ, rūpasmiṁ vā attānaṁ,
ne36:4.8	vedanāvantaṁ …pe…
ne36:4.9	saññāvantaṁ …
ne36:4.10	saṅkhāravantaṁ …
ne36:4.11	viññāṇavantaṁ attānaṁ upagacchanti, attani vā viññāṇaṁ, viññāṇasmiṁ vā attānaṁ, ayaṁ vuccati vīsativatthukā sakkāyadiṭṭhi.
ne36:5.1	Tassā paṭipakkho lokuttarā sammādiṭṭhi, anvāyikā sammāsaṅkappo sammāvācā sammākammanto sammāājīvo sammāvāyāmo sammāsati sammāsamādhi, ayaṁ ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo.
ne36:5.2	Te tayo khandhā sīlakkhandho samādhikkhandho paññākkhandho.
ne36:5.3	Sīlakkhandho samādhikkhandho ca samatho, paññākkhandho vipassanā.
ne36:5.4	Tattha sakkāyo dukkhaṁ, sakkāyasamudayo dukkhasamudayo, sakkāyanirodho dukkhanirodho, ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā, imāni cattāri saccāni.
ne36:5.5	Dukkhaṁ pariññeyyaṁ, samudayo pahātabbo, maggo bhāvetabbo, nirodho sacchikātabbo.
ne36:6.1	Tattha ye rūpaṁ attato upagacchanti.
ne36:6.2	Vedanaṁ …pe…
ne36:6.3	saññaṁ …
ne36:6.4	saṅkhāre …
ne36:6.5	viññāṇaṁ attato upagacchanti.
ne36:6.6	Ime vuccanti “ucchedavādino”ti.
ne36:6.7	Ye rūpavantaṁ attānaṁ upagacchanti.
ne36:6.8	Attani vā rūpaṁ, rūpasmiṁ vā attānaṁ.
ne36:6.9	Ye vedanāvantaṁ …pe…
ne36:6.10	ye saññāvantaṁ …
ne36:6.11	ye saṅkhāravantaṁ …
ne36:6.12	ye viññāṇavantaṁ attānaṁ upagacchanti, attani vā viññāṇaṁ, viññāṇasmiṁ vā attānaṁ.
ne36:6.13	Ime vuccanti “sassatavādino”ti, tattha ucchedasassatavādā ubho antā, ayaṁ saṁsārapavatti.
ne36:6.14	Tassa paṭipakkho majjhimā paṭipadā ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo, ayaṁ saṁsāranivatti.
ne36:6.15	Tattha pavatti dukkhaṁ, tadabhisaṅgo taṇhā samudayo, taṇhānirodho dukkhanirodho, ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā, imāni cattāri saccāni.
ne36:6.16	Dukkhaṁ pariññeyyaṁ, samudayo pahātabbo, maggo bhāvetabbo, nirodho sacchikātabbo.
ne36:7.1	Tattha ucchedasassataṁ samāsato vīsativatthukā sakkāyadiṭṭhi, vitthārato dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni, tesaṁ paṭipakkho tecattālīsaṁ bodhipakkhiyā dhammā aṭṭha vimokkhā dasa kasiṇāyatanāni.
ne36:7.2	Dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni mohajālaṁ anādianidhanappavattaṁ.
ne36:7.3	Tecattālīsaṁ bodhipakkhiyā dhammā ñāṇavajiraṁ mohajālappadālanaṁ.
ne36:7.4	Tattha moho avijjā, jālaṁ bhavataṇhā, tena vuccati “pubbā koṭi na paññāyati avijjāya ca bhavataṇhāya cā”ti.
ne36:8.1	Tattha diṭṭhicarito asmiṁ sāsane pabbajito sallekhānusantatavutti bhavati sallekhe tibbagāravo.
ne36:8.2	Taṇhācarito asmiṁ sāsane pabbajito sikkhānusantatavutti bhavati sikkhāya tibbagāravo.
ne36:8.3	Diṭṭhicarito sammattaniyāmaṁ okkamanto dhammānusārī bhavati.
ne36:8.4	Taṇhācarito sammattaniyāmaṁ okkamanto saddhānusārī bhavati, diṭṭhicarito sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiññāya khippābhiññāya ca niyyāti.
ne36:8.5	Taṇhācarito dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiññāya khippābhiññāya ca niyyāti.
ne36:9.1	Tattha kiṅkāraṇaṁ, yaṁ taṇhācarito dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiññāya khippābhiññāya ca niyyāti, tassa hi kāmā apariccattā bhavanti, so kāmehi viveciyamāno dukkhena paṭinissarati dandhañca dhammaṁ ājānāti?
ne36:9.2	Yo panāyaṁ diṭṭhicarito ayaṁ āditoyeva kāmehi anatthiko bhavati.
ne36:9.3	So tato viveciyamāno khippañca paṭinissarati, khippañca dhammaṁ ājānāti.
ne36:9.4	Dukkhāpi paṭipadā duvidhā dandhābhiññā ca khippābhiññā ca.
ne36:9.5	Sukhāpi paṭipadā duvidhā dandhābhiññā ca khippābhiññā ca.
ne36:9.6	Sattāpi duvidhā mudindriyāpi tikkhindriyāpi.
ne36:9.7	Ye mudindriyā, te dandhañca paṭinissaranti dandhañca dhammaṁ ājānanti.
ne36:9.8	Ye tikkhindriyā, te khippañca paṭinissaranti, khippañca dhammaṁ ājānanti, imā catasso paṭipadā.
ne36:9.9	Ye hi keci niyyiṁsu vā niyyanti vā niyyissanti vā, te imāhi eva catūhi paṭipadāhi.
ne36:9.10	Evaṁ ariyā catukkamaggaṁ paññāpenti abudhajanasevitāya bālakantāya rattavāsiniyā nandiyā bhavataṇhāya avaṭṭanatthaṁ.
ne36:9.11	Ayaṁ vuccati nandiyāvaṭṭassa nayassa bhūmīti, tenāha “taṇhañca avijjampi ca samathenā”ti.
ne36:10.1	Veyyākaraṇesu hi ye kusalākusalāti te duvidhā upaparikkhitabbā—
ne36:10.2	lokavaṭṭānusārī ca lokavivaṭṭānusārī ca.
ne36:10.3	Vaṭṭaṁ nāma saṁsāro.
ne36:10.4	Vivaṭṭaṁ nibbānaṁ.
ne36:10.5	Kammakilesā hetu saṁsārassa.
ne36:10.6	Tattha kammaṁ cetanā cetasikañca niddisitabbaṁ.
ne36:10.7	Taṁ kathaṁ daṭṭhabbaṁ?
ne36:10.8	Upacayena sabbepi kilesā catūhi vipallāsehi niddisitabbā.
ne36:10.9	Te kattha daṭṭhabbā?
ne36:10.10	Dasa vatthuke kilesapuñje.
ne36:10.11	Katamāni dasa vatthūni?
ne36:10.12	Cattāro āhārā, cattāro vipallāsā, cattāri upādānāni, cattāro yogā, cattāro ganthā, cattāro āsavā, cattāro oghā, cattāro sallā, catasso viññāṇaṭṭhitiyo cattāri agatigamanāni.
ne36:10.13	Paṭhame āhāre paṭhamo vipallāso, dutiye āhāre dutiyo vipallāso, tatiye āhāre tatiyo vipallāso, catutthe āhāre catuttho vipallāso.
ne36:10.14	Paṭhame vipallāse paṭhamaṁ upādānaṁ.
ne36:10.15	Dutiye vipallāse dutiyaṁ upādānaṁ, tatiye vipallāse tatiyaṁ upādānaṁ, catutthe vipallāse catutthaṁ upādānaṁ.
ne36:10.16	Paṭhame upādāne paṭhamo yogo, dutiye upādāne dutiyo yogo, tatiye upādāne tatiyo yogo, catutthe upādāne catuttho yogo.
ne36:10.17	Paṭhame yoge paṭhamo gantho, dutiye yoge dutiyo gantho, tatiye yoge tatiyo gantho, catutthe yoge catuttho gantho.
ne36:10.18	Paṭhame ganthe paṭhamo āsavo, dutiye ganthe dutiyo āsavo, tatiye ganthe tatiyo āsavo, catutthe ganthe catuttho āsavo.
ne36:10.19	Paṭhame āsave paṭhamo ogho, dutiye āsave dutiyo ogho, tatiye āsave tatiyo ogho, catutthe āsave catuttho ogho.
ne36:10.20	Paṭhame oghe paṭhamo sallo, dutiye oghe dutiyo sallo, tatiye oghe tatiyo sallo, catutthe oghe catuttho sallo.
ne36:10.21	Paṭhame salle paṭhamā viññāṇaṭṭhiti, dutiye salle dutiyā viññāṇaṭṭhiti, tatiye salle tatiyā viññāṇaṭṭhiti, catutthe salle catutthī viññāṇaṭṭhiti.
ne36:10.22	Paṭhamāyaṁ viññāṇaṭṭhitiyaṁ paṭhamaṁ agatigamanaṁ, dutiyāyaṁ viññāṇaṭṭhitiyaṁ dutiyaṁ agatigamanaṁ, tatiyāyaṁ viññāṇaṭṭhitiyaṁ tatiyaṁ agatigamanaṁ, catutthiyaṁ viññāṇaṭṭhitiyaṁ catutthaṁ agatigamanaṁ.
ne36:11.1	Tattha yo ca kabaḷīkāro āhāro phasso āhāro, ime taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.2	Yo ca manosañcetanāhāro yo ca viññāṇāhāro, ime diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.3	Tattha yo ca “asubhe subhan”ti vipallāso, yo ca “dukkhe sukhan”ti vipallāso, ime taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.4	Yo ca “anicce niccan”ti vipallāso, yo ca “anattani attā”ti vipallāso, ime diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.5	Tattha yañca kāmupādānaṁ yañca bhavupādānaṁ, ime taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.6	Yañca diṭṭhupādānaṁ yañca attavādupādānaṁ, ime diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.7	Tattha yo ca kāmayogo, yo ca bhavayogo, ime taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.8	Yo ca diṭṭhiyogo, yo ca avijjāyogo, ime diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.9	Tattha yo ca abhijjhākāyagantho, yo ca byāpādo kāyagantho, ime taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.10	Yo ca parāmāsakāyagantho, yo ca idaṁsaccābhinivesakāyagantho, ime diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.11	Tattha yo ca kāmāsavo, yo ca bhavāsavo, ime taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.12	Yo ca diṭṭhāsavo, yo ca avijjāsavo, ime diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.13	Tattha yo ca kāmogho, yo ca bhavogho, ime taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.14	Yo ca diṭṭhogho, yo ca avijjogho, ime diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.15	Tattha yo ca rāgasallo, yo ca dosasallo, ime taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.16	Yo ca mānasallo, yo ca mohasallo, ime diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.17	Tattha yā ca rūpūpagā viññāṇaṭṭhiti, yā ca vedanūpagā viññāṇaṭṭhiti, ime taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.18	Yā ca saññūpagā viññāṇaṭṭhiti, yā ca saṅkhārūpagā viññāṇaṭṭhiti, ime diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.19	Tattha yañca chandā agatigamanaṁ yañca dosā agatigamanaṁ, ime taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:11.20	Yañca bhayā agatigamanaṁ, yañca mohā agatigamanaṁ, ime diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:12.1	Tattha kabaḷīkāre āhāre “asubhe subhan”ti vipallāso, phasse āhāre “dukkhe sukhan”ti vipallāso, viññāṇe āhāre “anicce niccan”ti vipallāso, manosañcetanāya āhāre “anattani attā”ti vipallāso.
ne36:12.2	Paṭhame vipallāse ṭhito kāme upādiyati, idaṁ vuccati kāmupādānaṁ;
ne36:12.3	dutiye vipallāse ṭhito anāgataṁ bhavaṁ upādiyati, idaṁ vuccati bhavupādānaṁ;
ne36:12.4	tatiye vipallāse ṭhito saṁsārābhinandiniṁ diṭṭhiṁ upādiyati, idaṁ vuccati diṭṭhupādānaṁ;
ne36:12.5	catutthe vipallāse ṭhito attānaṁ kappiyaṁ upādiyati, idaṁ vuccati attavādupādānaṁ.
ne36:13.1	Kāmupādānena kāmehi saṁyujjati, ayaṁ vuccati kāmayogo;
ne36:13.2	bhavupādānena bhavehi saṁyujjati, ayaṁ vuccati bhavayogo;
ne36:13.3	diṭṭhupādānena pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā saṁyujjati, ayaṁ vuccati diṭṭhiyogo;
ne36:13.4	attavādupādānena avijjāya saṁyujjati, ayaṁ vuccati avijjāyogo.
ne36:14.1	Paṭhame yoge ṭhito abhijjhāya kāyaṁ ganthati, ayaṁ vuccati abhijjhākāyagantho;
ne36:14.2	dutiye yoge ṭhito byāpādena kāyaṁ ganthati, ayaṁ vuccati byāpādakāyagantho;
ne36:14.3	tatiye yoge ṭhito parāmāsena kāyaṁ ganthati, ayaṁ vuccati parāmāsakāyagantho;
ne36:14.4	catutthe yoge ṭhito idaṁsaccābhinivesena kāyaṁ ganthati, ayaṁ vuccati idaṁsaccābhinivesakāyagantho.
ne36:15.1	Tassa evaṅganthitā kilesā āsavanti.
ne36:15.2	Kuto ca vuccati āsavantīti?
ne36:15.3	Anusayato vā pariyuṭṭhānato vā.
ne36:15.4	Tattha abhijjhākāyaganthena kāmāsavo, byāpādakāyaganthena bhavāsavo, parāmāsakāyaganthena diṭṭhāsavo, idaṁsaccābhinivesakāyaganthena avijjāsavo.
ne36:16.1	Tassa ime cattāro āsavā vepullaṁ gatā oghā bhavanti.
ne36:16.2	Iti āsavavepullā oghavepullaṁ.
ne36:16.3	Tattha kāmāsavena kāmogho, bhavāsavena bhavogho, diṭṭhāsavena diṭṭhogho, avijjāsavena avijjogho.
ne36:17.1	Tassa ime cattāro oghā anusayasahagatā ajjhāsayaṁ anupaviṭṭhā hadayaṁ āhacca tiṭṭhanti, tena vuccanti sallāiti.
ne36:17.2	Tattha kāmoghena rāgasallo, bhavoghena dosasallo, diṭṭhoghena mānasallo, avijjoghena mohasallo.
ne36:18.1	Tassa imehi catūhi sallehi pariyādinnaṁ viññāṇaṁ catūsu dhammesu saṇṭhahati rūpe vedanāya saññāya saṅkhāresu.
ne36:18.2	Tattha rāgasallena nandūpasecanena viññāṇena rūpūpagā viññāṇaṭṭhiti, dosasallena nandūpasecanena viññāṇena vedanūpagā viññāṇaṭṭhiti, mānasallena nandūpasecanena viññāṇena saññūpagā viññāṇaṭṭhiti, mohasallena nandūpasecanena viññāṇena saṅkhārūpagā viññāṇaṭṭhiti.
ne36:19.1	Tassa imāhi catūhi viññāṇaṭṭhitīhi upatthaddhaṁ viññāṇaṁ catūhi dhammehi agatiṁ gacchati chandā dosā bhayā mohā.
ne36:19.2	Tattha rāgena chandāgatiṁ gacchati, dosena dosāgatiṁ gacchati, bhayena bhayāgatiṁ gacchati, mohena mohāgatiṁ gacchati.
ne36:19.3	Iti kho tañca kammaṁ ime ca kilesā, esa hetu saṁsārassa, evaṁ sabbe kilesā catūhi vipallāsehi niddisitabbā.
ne36:20.1	Tattha imā catasso disā kabaḷīkāro āhāro “asubhe subhan”ti vipallāso, kāmupādānaṁ, kāmayogo, abhijjhākāyagantho, kāmāsavo, kāmogho, rāgasallo, rūpūpagā viññāṇaṭṭhiti, chandā agatigamananti paṭhamā disā.
ne36:21.1	Phasso āhāro “dukkhe sukhan”ti vipallāso, bhavupādānaṁ, bhavayogo, byāpādakāyagantho, bhavāsavo, bhavogho, dosasallo, vedanūpagā viññāṇaṭṭhiti, dosā agatigamananti dutiyā disā.
ne36:22.1	Viññāṇāhāro “anicce niccan”ti vipallāso, diṭṭhupādānaṁ, diṭṭhiyogo parāmāsakāyagantho, diṭṭhāsavo, diṭṭhogho, mānasallo, saññūpagā viññāṇaṭṭhiti, bhayā agatigamananti tatiyā disā.
ne36:23.1	Manosañcetanāhāro “anattani attā”ti vipallāso, attavādupādānaṁ, avijjāyogo, idaṁsaccābhinivesakāyagantho, avijjāsavo, avijjogho, mohasallo, saṅkhārūpagā viññāṇaṭṭhiti, mohā agatigamananti catutthī disā.
ne36:24.1	Tattha yo ca kabaḷīkāro āhāro yo ca “asubhe subhan”ti vipallāso, kāmupādānaṁ, kāmayogo, abhijjhākāyagantho, kāmāsavo, kāmogho, rāgasallo, rūpūpagā viññāṇaṭṭhiti chandā agatigamananti, imesaṁ dasannaṁ suttānaṁ eko attho, byañjanameva nānaṁ.
ne36:24.2	Ime rāgacaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:25.1	Tattha yo ca phasso āhāro yo ca “dukkhe sukhan”ti vipallāso, bhavupādānaṁ, bhavayogo, byāpādakāyagantho, bhavāsavo, bhavogho, dosasallo, vedanūpagā viññāṇaṭṭhiti, dosā agatigamananti imesaṁ dasannaṁ suttānaṁ eko attho byañjanameva nānaṁ, ime dosacaritassa puggalassa upakkilesā.
ne36:26.1	Tattha yo ca viññāṇāhāro yo ca “anicce niccan”ti vipallāso, diṭṭhupādānaṁ, diṭṭhiyogo, parāmāsakāyagantho, diṭṭhāsavo, diṭṭhogho, mānasallo, saññūpagā viññāṇaṭṭhiti, bhayā agatigamananti imesaṁ dasannaṁ suttānaṁ eko attho, byañjanameva nānaṁ.
ne36:26.2	Ime diṭṭhicaritassa mandassa upakkilesā.
ne36:27.1	Tattha yo ca manosañcetanāhāro yo ca “anattani attā”ti vipallāso, attavādupādānaṁ, avijjāyogo, idaṁsaccābhinivesakāyagantho, avijjāsavo, avijjogho, mohasallo, saṅkhārūpagā viññāṇaṭṭhiti, mohā agatigamananti, imesaṁ dasannaṁ suttānaṁ eko attho, byañjanameva nānaṁ.
ne36:27.2	Ime diṭṭhicaritassa udattassa upakkilesā.
ne36:28.1	Tattha yo ca kabaḷīkāro āhāro yo ca phasso āhāro, ime appaṇihitena vimokkhamukhena pariññaṁ gacchanti, viññāṇāhāro suññatāya, manosañcetanāhāro animittena, tattha yo ca “asubhe subhan”ti vipallāso, yo ca “dukkhe sukhan”ti vipallāso, ime appaṇihitena vimokkhamukhena pahānaṁ abbhatthaṁ gacchanti.
ne36:28.2	“Anicce niccan”ti vipallāso suññatāya, “anattani attā”ti vipallāso animittena.
ne36:28.3	Tattha kāmupādānañca bhavupādānañca appaṇihitena vimokkhamukhena pahānaṁ gacchanti.
ne36:28.4	Diṭṭhupādānaṁ suññatāya, attavādupādānaṁ animittena.
ne36:28.5	Tattha kāmayogo ca bhavayogo ca appaṇihitena vimokkhamukhena pahānaṁ gacchanti, diṭṭhiyogo suññatāya, avijjāyogo animittena.
ne36:28.6	Tattha abhijjhākāyagantho ca byāpādakāyagantho ca appaṇihitena vimokkhamukhena pahānaṁ gacchanti, parāmāsakāyagantho suññatāya, idaṁsaccābhinivesakāyagantho animittena.
ne36:29.1	Tattha kāmāsavo ca bhavāsavo ca appaṇihitena vimokkhamukhena pahānaṁ gacchanti, diṭṭhāsavo suññatāya, avijjāsavo animittena.
ne36:29.2	Tattha kāmogho ca bhavogho ca appaṇihitena vimokkhamukhena pahānaṁ gacchanti, diṭṭhogho suññatāya, avijjogho animittena.
ne36:29.3	Tattha rāgasallo ca dosasallo ca appaṇihitena vimokkhamukhena pahānaṁ gacchanti, mānasallo suññatāya, mohasallo animittena.
ne36:29.4	Tattha rūpūpagā ca viññāṇaṭṭhiti vedanūpagā ca viññāṇaṭṭhiti appaṇihitena vimokkhamukhena pariññaṁ gacchanti, saññūpagā suññatāya, saṅkhārūpagā animittena.
ne36:30.1	Tattha chandā ca agatigamanaṁ dosā ca agatigamanaṁ appaṇihitena vimokkhamukhena pahānaṁ gacchanti, bhayā agatigamanaṁ suññatāya, mohā agatigamanaṁ animittena vimokkhamukhena pahānaṁ gacchanti.
ne36:30.2	Iti sabbe lokavaṭṭānusārino dhammā niyyanti.
ne36:30.3	Te lokā tīhi vimokkhamukhehi.
ne36:31.1	Tatridaṁ niyyānaṁ—
ne36:32.1	Catasso paṭipadā, cattāro satipaṭṭhānā, cattāri jhānāni, cattāro vihārā, cattāro sammappadhānā, cattāro acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, cattāri adhiṭṭhānāni, catasso samādhibhāvanā, cattāro sukhabhāgiyā dhammā, catasso appamāṇā.
ne36:33.1	Paṭhamā paṭipadā paṭhamaṁ satipaṭṭhānaṁ, dutiyā paṭipadā dutiyaṁ satipaṭṭhānaṁ, tatiyā paṭipadā tatiyaṁ satipaṭṭhānaṁ, catutthī paṭipadā catutthaṁ satipaṭṭhānaṁ.
ne36:33.2	Paṭhamaṁ satipaṭṭhānaṁ paṭhamaṁ jhānaṁ, dutiyaṁ satipaṭṭhānaṁ dutiyaṁ jhānaṁ, tatiyaṁ satipaṭṭhānaṁ tatiyaṁ jhānaṁ, catutthaṁ satipaṭṭhānaṁ catutthaṁ jhānaṁ.
ne36:33.3	Paṭhamaṁ jhānaṁ paṭhamo vihāro, dutiyaṁ jhānaṁ dutiyo vihāro, tatiyaṁ jhānaṁ tatiyo vihāro, catutthaṁ jhānaṁ catuttho vihāro.
ne36:33.4	Paṭhamo vihāro paṭhamaṁ sammappadhānaṁ, dutiyo vihāro dutiyaṁ sammappadhānaṁ, tatiyo vihāro tatiyaṁ sammappadhānaṁ, catuttho vihāro catutthaṁ sammappadhānaṁ.
ne36:33.5	Paṭhamaṁ sammappadhānaṁ paṭhamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, dutiyaṁ dutiyo, tatiyaṁ tatiyo, catutthaṁ sammappadhānaṁ catuttho acchariyo abbhuto dhammo.
ne36:33.6	Paṭhamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo paṭhamaṁ adhiṭṭhānaṁ, dutiyo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo dutiyaṁ adhiṭṭhānaṁ, tatiyo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo tatiyaṁ adhiṭṭhānaṁ, catuttho acchariyo abbhuto dhammo catutthaṁ adhiṭṭhānaṁ.
ne36:33.7	Paṭhamaṁ adhiṭṭhānaṁ paṭhamā samādhibhāvanā, dutiyaṁ adhiṭṭhānaṁ dutiyā samādhibhāvanā, tatiyaṁ adhiṭṭhānaṁ tatiyā samādhibhāvanā, catutthaṁ adhiṭṭhānaṁ catutthī samādhibhāvanā.
ne36:33.8	Paṭhamā samādhibhāvanā paṭhamo sukhabhāgiyo dhammo, dutiyā samādhibhāvanā dutiyo sukhabhāgiyo dhammo, tatiyā samādhibhāvanā tatiyo sukhabhāgiyo dhammo, catutthī samādhibhāvanā catuttho sukhabhāgiyo dhammo.
ne36:33.9	Paṭhamo sukhabhāgiyo dhammo paṭhamaṁ appamāṇaṁ, dutiyo sukhabhāgiyo dhammo dutiyaṁ appamāṇaṁ, tatiyo sukhabhāgiyo dhammo tatiyaṁ appamāṇaṁ, catuttho sukhabhāgiyo dhammo catutthaṁ appamāṇaṁ.
ne36:33.10	Paṭhamā paṭipadā bhāvitā bahulīkatā paṭhamaṁ satipaṭṭhānaṁ paripūreti, dutiyā paṭipadā bhāvitā bahulīkatā dutiyaṁ satipaṭṭhānaṁ paripūreti, tatiyā paṭipadā bhāvitā bahulīkatā tatiyaṁ satipaṭṭhānaṁ paripūreti, catutthī paṭipadā bhāvitā bahulīkatā catutthaṁ satipaṭṭhānaṁ paripūreti.
ne36:33.11	Paṭhamo satipaṭṭhāno bhāvito bahulīkato paṭhamaṁ jhānaṁ paripūreti, dutiyo satipaṭṭhāno bhāvito bahulīkato dutiyaṁ jhānaṁ paripūreti, tatiyo satipaṭṭhāno bhāvito bahulīkato tatiyaṁ jhānaṁ paripūreti, catuttho satipaṭṭhāno bhāvito bahulīkato catutthaṁ jhānaṁ paripūreti.
ne36:34.1	Paṭhamaṁ jhānaṁ bhāvitaṁ bahulīkataṁ paṭhamaṁ vihāraṁ paripūreti, dutiyaṁ jhānaṁ bhāvitaṁ bahulīkataṁ dutiyaṁ vihāraṁ paripūreti, tatiyaṁ jhānaṁ bhāvitaṁ bahulīkataṁ tatiyaṁ vihāraṁ paripūreti, catutthaṁ jhānaṁ bhāvitaṁ bahulīkataṁ catutthaṁ vihāraṁ paripūreti.
ne36:34.2	Paṭhamo vihāro bhāvito bahulīkato anuppannānaṁ pāpakānaṁ akusalānaṁ dhammānaṁ anuppādaṁ paripūreti, dutiyo vihāro bhāvito bahulīkato uppannānaṁ pāpakānaṁ akusalānaṁ dhammānaṁ pahānaṁ paripūreti, tatiyo vihāro bhāvito bahulīkato anuppannānaṁ kusalānaṁ dhammānaṁ uppādaṁ paripūreti, catuttho vihāro bhāvito bahulīkato uppannānaṁ kusalānaṁ dhammānaṁ ṭhitiṁ asammosaṁ bhiyyobhāvaṁ paripūreti.
ne36:34.3	Paṭhamaṁ sammappadhānaṁ bhāvitaṁ bahulīkataṁ mānappahānaṁ paripūreti, dutiyaṁ sammappadhānaṁ bhāvitaṁ bahulīkataṁ ālayasamugghātaṁ paripūreti, tatiyaṁ sammappadhānaṁ bhāvitaṁ bahulīkataṁ avijjāpahānaṁ paripūreti, catutthaṁ sammappadhānaṁ bhāvitaṁ bahulīkataṁ bhavūpasamaṁ paripūreti.
ne36:34.4	Mānappahānaṁ bhāvitaṁ bahulīkataṁ saccādhiṭṭhānaṁ paripūreti, ālayasamugghāto bhāvito bahulīkato cāgādhiṭṭhānaṁ paripūreti, avijjāpahānaṁ bhāvitaṁ bahulīkataṁ paññādhiṭṭhānaṁ paripūreti, bhavūpasamo bhāvito bahulīkato upasamādhiṭṭhānaṁ paripūreti.
ne36:34.5	Saccādhiṭṭhānaṁ bhāvitaṁ bahulīkataṁ chandasamādhiṁ paripūreti, cāgādhiṭṭhānaṁ bhāvitaṁ bahulīkataṁ vīriyasamādhiṁ paripūreti, paññādhiṭṭhānaṁ bhāvitaṁ bahulīkataṁ cittasamādhiṁ paripūreti, upasamādhiṭṭhānaṁ bhāvitaṁ bahulīkataṁ vīmaṁsāsamādhiṁ paripūreti.
ne36:34.6	Chandasamādhi bhāvito bahulīkato indriyasaṁvaraṁ paripūreti, vīriyasamādhi bhāvito bahulīkato tapaṁ paripūreti, cittasamādhi bhāvito bahulīkato buddhiṁ paripūreti, vīmaṁsāsamādhi bhāvito bahulīkato sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggaṁ paripūreti.
ne36:34.7	Indriyasaṁvaro bhāvito bahulīkato mettaṁ paripūreti, tapo bhāvito bahulīkato karuṇaṁ paripūreti, buddhi bhāvitā bahulīkatā muditaṁ paripūreti, sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo bhāvito bahulīkato upekkhaṁ paripūreti.
ne36:35.1	Tattha imā catasso disā paṭhamā paṭipadā paṭhamo satipaṭṭhāno paṭhamaṁ jhānaṁ paṭhamo vihāro paṭhamo sammappadhāno paṭhamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo saccādhiṭṭhānaṁ chandasamādhi indriyasaṁvaro mettā iti paṭhamā disā.
ne36:36.1	Dutiyā paṭipadā dutiyo satipaṭṭhāno dutiyaṁ jhānaṁ dutiyo vihāro dutiyo sammappadhāno dutiyo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo bhavādhiṭṭhānaṁ vīriyasamādhi tapo karuṇā iti dutiyā disā.
ne36:37.1	Tatiyā paṭipadā tatiyo satipaṭṭhāno tatiyaṁ jhānaṁ tatiyo vihāro tatiyo sammappadhāno tatiyo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo paññādhiṭṭhānaṁ cittasamādhi buddhi muditā iti tatiyā disā.
ne36:38.1	Catutthī paṭipadā catuttho satipaṭṭhāno catutthaṁ jhānaṁ catuttho vihāro catuttho sammappadhāno catuttho acchariyo abbhuto dhammo upasamādhiṭṭhānaṁ vīmaṁsāsamādhi sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo upekkhā iti catutthī disā.
ne36:39.1	Tattha paṭhamā paṭipadā paṭhamo satipaṭṭhāno paṭhamaṁ jhānaṁ paṭhamo vihāro paṭhamo sammappadhāno paṭhamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo saccādhiṭṭhānaṁ chandasamādhi indriyasaṁvaro, mettā iti imesaṁ dasannaṁ suttānaṁ eko attho, byañjanameva nānaṁ.
ne36:39.2	Idaṁ rāgacaritassa puggalassa bhesajjaṁ.
ne36:40.1	Dutiyā paṭipadā dutiyo satipaṭṭhāno dutiyaṁ jhānaṁ dutiyo vihāro dutiyo sammappadhāno dutiyo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo cāgādhiṭṭhānaṁ vīriyasamādhi tapo karuṇā iti imesaṁ dasannaṁ suttānaṁ eko attho, byañjanameva nānaṁ.
ne36:40.2	Idaṁ dosacaritassa puggalassa bhesajjaṁ.
ne36:41.1	Tatiyā paṭipadā tatiyo satipaṭṭhāno tatiyaṁ jhānaṁ tatiyo vihāro tatiyo sammappadhāno tatiyo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo paññādhiṭṭhānaṁ cittasamādhi buddhi muditā iti imesaṁ dasannaṁ suttānaṁ eko attho, byañjanameva nānaṁ.
ne36:41.2	Idaṁ diṭṭhicaritassa mandassa bhesajjaṁ.
ne36:42.1	Catutthī paṭipadā catuttho satipaṭṭhāno catutthaṁ jhānaṁ catuttho vihāro catuttho sammappadhāno catuttho acchariyo abbhuto dhammo upasamādhiṭṭhānaṁ vīmaṁsāsamādhi sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo upekkhā iti imesaṁ dasannaṁ suttānaṁ eko attho, byañjanameva nānaṁ.
ne36:42.2	Idaṁ diṭṭhicaritassa udattassa bhesajjaṁ.
ne36:43.1	Tattha dukkhā ca paṭipadā dandhābhiññā dukkhā ca paṭipadā khippābhiññā appaṇihitaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiññā suññataṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiññā animittaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ.
ne36:44.1	Tattha kāye kāyānupassitā satipaṭṭhānañca vedanāsu vedanānupassitā satipaṭṭhānañca appaṇihitaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ, citte cittānupassitā suññataṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ.
ne36:44.2	Dhammesu dhammānupassitā animittaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ.
ne36:45.1	Tattha paṭhamañca jhānaṁ dutiyañca jhānaṁ appaṇihitaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ, tatiyaṁ jhānaṁ suññataṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ, catutthaṁ jhānaṁ animittaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ.
ne36:46.1	Tattha paṭhamo ca vihāro dutiyo ca vihāro appaṇihitaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ, tatiyo vihāro suññataṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ, catuttho vihāro animittaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ.
ne36:47.1	Yattha paṭhamañca sammappadhānaṁ dutiyañca sammappadhānaṁ appaṇihitaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ, tatiyaṁ sammappadhānaṁ suññataṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ, catutthaṁ sammappadhānaṁ animittaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ.
ne36:48.1	Tattha mānappahānañca ālayasamugghāto ca appaṇihitaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ, avijjāpahānaṁ suññataṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ, bhavūpasamo animittaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ.
ne36:49.1	Tattha saccādhiṭṭhānañca cāgādhiṭṭhānañca appaṇihitaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ, paññādhiṭṭhānaṁ suññataṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ, upasamādhiṭṭhānaṁ animittaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ.
ne36:50.1	Tattha chandasamādhi ca vīriyasamādhi ca appaṇihitaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ, cittasamādhi suññataṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ, vīmaṁsāsamādhi animittaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ.
ne36:51.1	Tattha indriyasaṁvaro ca tapo ca appaṇihitaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ, buddhi suññataṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo animittaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ.
ne36:52.1	Tattha mettā ca karuṇā ca appaṇihitaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ, muditā suññataṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ upekkhā animittaṁ vimokkhamukhaṁ.
ne36:53.1	Tesaṁ vikkīḷitaṁ.
ne36:53.2	Cattāro āhārā tesaṁ paṭipakkho catasso paṭipadā …pe…
ne36:53.3	cattāro vipallāsā tesaṁ paṭipakkho cattāro satipaṭṭhānā.
ne36:53.4	Cattāri upādānāni tesaṁ paṭipakkho cattāri jhānāni.
ne36:53.5	Cattāro yogā tesaṁ paṭipakkho cattāro vihārā.
ne36:53.6	Cattāro ganthā tesaṁ paṭipakkho cattāro sammappadhānā.
ne36:53.7	Cattāro āsavā tesaṁ paṭipakkho cattāro acchariyā abbhutā dhammā.
ne36:53.8	Cattāro oghā tesaṁ paṭipakkho cattāri adhiṭṭhānāni.
ne36:53.9	Cattāro sallā tesaṁ paṭipakkho catasso samādhibhāvanā.
ne36:53.10	Catasso viññāṇaṭṭhitiyo tāsaṁ paṭipakkho cattāro sukhabhāgiyā dhammā.
ne36:53.11	Cattāri agatigamanāni tesaṁ paṭipakkho catasso appamāṇā.
ne36:54.1	Sīhā buddhā paccekabuddhā sāvakā ca hatarāgadosamohā, tesaṁ vikkīḷitaṁ bhāvanā sacchikiriyā byantīkiriyā ca.
ne36:54.2	Vikkīḷitaṁ indriyādhiṭṭhānaṁ vikkīḷitaṁ vipariyāsānadhiṭṭhānañca.
ne36:54.3	Indriyāni saddhammagocaro vipariyāsā kilesagocaro.
ne36:54.4	Ayaṁ vuccati sīhavikkīḷitassa ca nayassa disālocanassa ca nayassa bhūmīti.
ne36:54.5	Tenāha “yo neti vipallāsehi saṅkilese”ti.
ne36:54.6	Veyyākaraṇesu hi ye “kusalākusalā”ti ca.
ne36:55.1	Tattha ye dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiññāya khippābhiññāya ca niyyanti, ime dve puggalā;
ne36:55.2	ye sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiññāya khippābhiññāya ca niyyanti, ime dve puggalā.
ne36:55.3	Tesaṁ catunnaṁ puggalānaṁ ayaṁ saṅkileso, cattāro āhārā, cattāro vipallāsā, cattāri upādānāni, cattāro yogā, cattāro ganthā, cattāro āsavā, cattāro oghā, cattāro sallā, catasso viññāṇaṭṭhitiyo, cattāri agatigamanānīti.
ne36:55.4	Tesaṁ catunnaṁ puggalānaṁ idaṁ vodānaṁ, catasso paṭipadā, cattāro satipaṭṭhānā, cattāri jhānāni, cattāro vihārā, cattāro sammappadhānā, cattāro acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, cattāri adhiṭṭhānāni, catasso samādhibhāvanā, cattāro sukhabhāgiyā dhammā, catasso appamāṇā iti.
ne36:56.1	Tattha ye dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiññāya khippābhiññāya ca niyyanti ime dve puggalā.
ne36:56.2	Ye sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiññāya khippābhiññāya ca niyyanti, ime dve puggalā.
ne36:56.3	Tattha yo sukhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiññāya niyyāti, ayaṁ ugghaṭitaññū.
ne36:56.4	Yo sādhāraṇāya, ayaṁ vipañcitaññū.
ne36:56.5	Yo dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiññāya niyyāti, ayaṁ neyyo.
ne36:57.1	Tattha bhagavā ugghaṭitaññussa puggalassa samathaṁ upadisati, neyyassa vipassanaṁ, samathavipassanaṁ vipañcitaññussa.
ne36:57.2	Tattha bhagavā ugghaṭitaññussa puggalassa mudukaṁ dhammadesanaṁ upadisati, tikkhaṁ neyyassa, mudutikkhaṁ vipañcitaññussa, tattha bhagavā ugghaṭitaññussa puggalassa saṅkhittena dhammaṁ desayati, saṅkhittavitthārena vipañcitaññussa, vitthārena neyyassa.
ne36:57.3	Tattha bhagavā ugghaṭitaññussa puggalassa nissaraṇaṁ upadisati, vipañcitaññussa ādīnavañca nissaraṇañca upadisati, neyyassa assādañca ādīnavañca nissaraṇañca upadisati.
ne36:57.4	Tattha bhagavā ugghaṭitaññussa adhipaññāsikkhaṁ paññāpayati, adhicittaṁ vipañcitaññussa, adhisīlaṁ neyyassa.
ne36:58.1	Tattha ye dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiññāya khippābhiññāya ca niyyanti, ime dve puggalā.
ne36:58.2	Ye sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiññāya khippābhiññāya ca niyyanti.
ne36:58.3	Ime dve puggalā.
ne36:58.4	Iti kho cattāri hutvā tīṇi bhavanti ugghaṭitaññū vipañcitaññū neyyoti.
ne36:59.1	Tesaṁ tiṇṇaṁ puggalānaṁ ayaṁ saṅkileso, tīṇi akusalamūlāni lobho akusalamūlaṁ doso akusalamūlaṁ moho akusalamūlaṁ, tīṇi duccaritāni—
ne36:59.2	kāyaduccaritaṁ vacīduccaritaṁ manoduccaritaṁ;
ne36:59.3	tayo akusalavitakkā—
ne36:59.4	kāmavitakko byāpādavitakko vihiṁsāvitakko;
ne36:59.5	tisso akusalasaññā—
ne36:59.6	kāmasaññā byāpādasaññā vihiṁsāsaññā;
ne36:59.7	tisso viparītasaññā—
ne36:59.8	niccasaññā sukhasaññā attasaññā;
ne36:59.9	tisso vedanā—
ne36:59.10	sukhā vedanā dukkhā vedanā adukkhamasukhā vedanā;
ne36:59.11	tisso dukkhatā—
ne36:59.12	dukkhadukkhatā saṅkhāradukkhatā vipariṇāmadukkhatā;
ne36:59.13	tayo aggī—
ne36:59.14	rāgaggi dosaggi mohaggi;
ne36:59.15	tayo sallā—
ne36:59.16	rāgasallo dosasallo mohasallo;
ne36:59.17	tisso jaṭā—
ne36:59.18	rāgajaṭā dosajaṭā mohajaṭā;
ne36:59.19	tisso akusalūpaparikkhā—
ne36:59.20	akusalaṁ kāyakammaṁ akusalaṁ vacīkammaṁ akusalaṁ manokammaṁ.
ne36:59.21	Tisso vipattiyo—
ne36:59.22	sīlavipatti diṭṭhivipatti ācāravipattīti.
ne36:59.23	Tesaṁ tiṇṇaṁ puggalānaṁ idaṁ vodānaṁ.
ne36:59.24	Tīṇi kusalamūlāni—
ne36:59.25	alobho kusalamūlaṁ adoso kusalamūlaṁ amoho kusalamūlaṁ.
ne36:59.26	Tīṇi sucaritāni—
ne36:59.27	kāyasucaritaṁ vacīsucaritaṁ manosucaritaṁ.
ne36:59.28	Tayo kusalavitakkā—
ne36:59.29	nekkhammavitakko abyāpādavitakko avihiṁsāvitakko.
ne36:59.30	Tayo samādhī—
ne36:59.31	savitakko savicāro samādhi avitakko vicāramatto samādhi avitakko avicāro samādhi.
ne36:59.32	Tisso kusalasaññā—
ne36:59.33	nekkhammasaññā abyāpādasaññā avihiṁsāsaññā.
ne36:59.34	Tisso aviparītasaññā—
ne36:59.35	aniccasaññā dukkhasaññā anattasaññā.
ne36:59.36	Tisso kusalūpaparikkhā—
ne36:59.37	kusalaṁ kāyakammaṁ kusalaṁ vacīkammaṁ kusalaṁ manokammaṁ.
ne36:59.38	Tīṇi soceyyāni—
ne36:59.39	kāyasoceyyaṁ vacīsoceyyaṁ manosoceyyaṁ;
ne36:59.40	tisso sampattiyo—
ne36:59.41	sīlasampatti samādhisampatti paññāsampatti.
ne36:59.42	Tisso sikkhā—
ne36:59.43	adhisīlasikkhā adhicittasikkhā adhipaññāsikkhā;
ne36:59.44	tayo khandhā—
ne36:59.45	sīlakkhandho samādhikkhandho paññākkhandho.
ne36:59.46	Tīṇi vimokkhamukhāni—
ne36:59.47	suññataṁ animittaṁ appaṇihitanti.
ne36:60.1	Iti kho cattāri hutvā tīṇi bhavanti, tīṇi hutvā dve bhavanti taṇhācarito ca diṭṭhicarito ca.
ne36:61.1	Tesaṁ dvinnaṁ puggalānaṁ ayaṁ saṅkileso, taṇhā ca avijjā ca ahirikañca anottappañca assati ca asampajaññañca ayoniso manasikāro ca kosajjañca dovacassañca ahaṅkāro ca mamaṅkāro ca assaddhā ca pamādo ca asaddhammassavanañca asaṁvaro ca abhijjhā ca byāpādo ca nīvaraṇañca saṁyojanañca kodho ca upanāho ca makkho ca palāso ca issā ca maccherañca māyā ca sāṭheyyañca sassatadiṭṭhi ca ucchedadiṭṭhi cāti.
ne36:62.1	Tesaṁ dvinnaṁ puggalānaṁ idaṁ vodānaṁ, samatho ca vipassanā ca hirī ca ottappañca sati ca sampajaññañca yoniso manasikāro ca vīriyārambho ca sovacassañca dhamme ñāṇañca anvaye ñāṇañca khaye ñāṇañca anuppāde ñāṇañca saddhā ca appamādo ca saddhammassavanañca saṁvaro ca anabhijjhā ca abyāpādo ca rāgavirāgā ca cetovimutti avijjāvirāgā ca paññāvimutti abhisamayo ca appicchatā ca santuṭṭhi ca akkodho ca anupanāho ca amakkho ca apalāso ca issāpahānañca macchariyappahānañca vijjā ca vimutti ca saṅkhatārammaṇo ca vimokkho asaṅkhatārammaṇo ca vimokkho saupādisesā ca nibbānadhātu anupādisesā ca nibbānadhātūti.
ne36:63.1	Ayaṁ vuccati tipukkhalassa ca nayassa aṅkusassa ca nayassa bhūmīti.
ne36:63.2	Tenāha “yo akusale samūlehi netī”ti “oloketvā disalocanenā”ti ca.
ne36:63.3	Niyuttaṁ nayasamuṭṭhānaṁ.
ne37:0.1	Netti
ne37:0.2	6. Sāsanapaṭṭhāna
ne37:1.1	Tattha aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā kuhiṁ daṭṭhabbā?
ne37:1.2	Sāsanapaṭṭhāne.
ne37:1.3	Tattha katamaṁ sāsanapaṭṭhānaṁ?

ne37:2.1	Tattha saṅkileso tividho—
ne37:2.2	taṇhāsaṅkileso diṭṭhisaṅkileso duccaritasaṅkileso.
ne37:2.3	Tattha taṇhāsaṅkileso samathena visujjhati, so samatho samādhikkhandho.
ne37:2.4	Diṭṭhisaṅkileso vipassanāya visujjhati, sā vipassanā paññākkhandho.
ne37:2.5	Duccaritasaṅkileso sucaritena visujjhati, taṁ sucaritaṁ sīlakkhandho.
ne37:2.6	Tassa sīle patiṭṭhitassa yadi āsatti uppajjati bhavesu, evaṁ sāyaṁ samathavipassanā bhāvanāmayaṁ puññakriyavatthu bhavati tatrūpapattiyā saṁvattati.
ne37:2.7	Imāni cattāri suttāni, sādhāraṇāni katāni aṭṭha bhavanti, tāniyeva aṭṭha suttāni sādhāraṇāni katāni soḷasa bhavanti.
ne37:3.1	Imehi soḷasahi suttehi bhinnehi navavidhaṁ suttaṁ bhinnaṁ bhavati.
ne37:3.2	Gāthāya gāthā anuminitabbā, veyyākaraṇena veyyākaraṇaṁ anuminitabbaṁ.
ne37:3.3	Suttena suttaṁ anuminitabbaṁ.

ne37:29.2	Imamhi cāyaṁ samaye kālaṁ kareyya, yathābhataṁ nikkhitto, evaṁ niraye.
ne37:29.3	Taṁ kissa hetu?
ne37:29.4	Cittaṁ hissa, bhikkhave, paduṭṭhaṁ, cetopadosahetu kho pana, bhikkhave, evam’idhekacce sattā kāyassa bhedā paraṁ maraṇā apāyaṁ duggatiṁ vinipātaṁ nirayaṁ upapajjantī”ti.
ne37:29.5	Etamatthaṁ bhagavā avoca, tatthetaṁ iti vuccati—
ne37:30.1	“Paduṭṭhacittaṁ ñatvāna,
ne37:30.2	ekaccaṁ idha puggalaṁ;
ne37:30.3	Etamatthañca byākāsi,
ne37:30.4	buddho bhikkhūna santike.
ne37:31.1	Imamhi cāyaṁ samaye,
ne37:31.2	kālaṁ kayirātha puggalo;
ne37:31.3	Nirayaṁ upapajjeyya,
ne37:31.4	cittaṁ hissa padūsitaṁ.
ne37:32.1	Cetopadosahetu hi,
ne37:32.2	sattā gacchanti duggatiṁ;
ne37:32.3	Yathābhataṁ nikkhipeyya,
ne37:32.4	evameva tathāvidho;
ne37:32.5	Kāyassa bhedā duppañño,
ne37:32.6	nirayaṁ sopapajjatī”ti.
ne37:33.1	Ayampi attho vutto bhagavatā iti me sutanti.
ne37:34.1	Idaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ.

ne37:35.4	āvi vā yadi vā raho.

ne37:66.1	Idaṁ vāsanābhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ.
ne37:67.1	Mahānāmo sakko bhagavantaṁ etadavoca—
ne37:67.2	“idaṁ, bhante, kapilavatthu iddhañceva phītañca bāhujaññaṁ ākiṇṇamanussaṁ sambādhabyūhaṁ, so kho ahaṁ, bhante, bhagavantaṁ vā payirupāsitvā manobhāvanīye vā bhikkhū sāyanhasamayaṁ kapilavatthuṁ pavisanto bhantenapi hatthinā samāgacchāmi, bhantenapi assena samāgacchāmi, bhantenapi rathena samāgacchāmi, bhantenapi sakaṭena samāgacchāmi, bhantenapi purisena samāgacchāmi, tassa mayhaṁ, bhante, tasmiṁ samaye mussateva bhagavantaṁ ārabbha sati, mussati dhammaṁ ārabbha sati, mussati saṅghaṁ ārabbha sati.
ne37:67.3	Tassa mayhaṁ, bhante, evaṁ hoti ‘imamhi cāhaṁ sāyanhasamaye kālaṁ kareyyaṁ, kā mayhaṁ gati, ko abhisamparāyo’”ti.
ne37:68.1	“Mā bhāyi, mahānāma, mā bhāyi, mahānāma, apāpakaṁ te maraṇaṁ bhavissati, apāpikā kālaṅkiriyā.
ne37:68.2	Catūhi kho, mahānāma, dhammehi samannāgato ariyasāvako nibbānaninno hoti nibbānapoṇo nibbānapabbhāro.
ne37:68.3	Katamehi catūhi?
ne37:68.4	Idha, mahānāma, ariyasāvako buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti, itipi so bhagavā arahaṁ …pe… buddho bhagavāti.
ne37:68.5	Dhamme …pe…
ne37:68.6	saṅghe …pe…
ne37:68.7	ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi …pe…
ne37:68.8	samādhisaṁvattanikehi.
ne37:68.9	Seyyathāpi, mahānāma, rukkho pācīnaninno pācīnapoṇo pācīnapabbhāro, so mūlacchinno katamena papateyyā”ti?
ne37:68.10	“Yena, bhante, ninno yena poṇo yena pabbhāro”ti.
ne37:68.11	“Evameva kho, mahānāma, imehi catūhi dhammehi samannāgato ariyasāvako nibbānaninno hoti nibbānapoṇo nibbānapabbhāro.
ne37:68.12	Mā bhāyi, mahānāma, mā bhāyi, mahānāma, apāpakaṁ te maraṇaṁ bhavissati, apāpikā kālaṅkiriyā”ti.

ne37:74.1	Idaṁ vāsanābhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ.
ne37:75.1	Bhagavā sāvatthiyaṁ viharati jetavane anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme.
ne37:75.2	Tena kho pana samayena sambahulā bhikkhū bhagavato cīvarakammaṁ karonti “niṭṭhitacīvaro bhagavā temāsaccayena cārikaṁ pakkamissatī”ti.
ne37:75.3	Tena kho pana samayena isidattapurāṇā thapatayo sākete paṭivasanti kenaci deva karaṇīyena.
ne37:75.4	Assosuṁ kho isidattapurāṇā thapatayo “sambahulā kira bhikkhū bhagavato cīvarakammaṁ karonti.
ne37:75.5	Niṭṭhitacīvaro bhagavā temāsaccayena cārikaṁ pakkamissatī”ti.
ne37:76.1	Atha kho isidattapurāṇā thapatayo magge purisaṁ ṭhapesuṁ “yadā tvaṁ, ambho purisa, passeyyāsi bhagavantaṁ āgacchantaṁ arahantaṁ sammāsambuddhaṁ, atha amhākaṁ āroceyyāsī”ti.
ne37:76.2	Dvīhatīhaṁ ṭhito kho so puriso addasa bhagavantaṁ dūratova āgacchantaṁ, disvāna yena isidattapurāṇā thapatayo tenupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā isidattapurāṇe thapatayo etadavoca—
ne37:76.3	“ayaṁ so, bhante bhagavā āgacchati arahaṁ sammāsambuddho, yassadāni kālaṁ maññathā”ti.
ne37:77.1	Atha kho isidattapurāṇā thapatayo yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkamiṁsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṁ abhivādetvā bhagavantaṁ piṭṭhito piṭṭhito anubandhiṁsu.
ne37:77.2	Atha kho bhagavā maggā okkamma yena aññataraṁ rukkhamūlaṁ tenupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi.
ne37:77.3	Isidattapurāṇā thapatayo bhagavantaṁ abhivādetvā ekamantaṁ nisīdiṁsu, ekamantaṁ nisinnā kho isidattapurāṇā thapatayo bhagavantaṁ etadavocuṁ—
ne37:78.1	“Yadā mayaṁ, bhante, bhagavantaṁ suṇoma ‘sāvatthiyā kosalesu cārikaṁ pakkamissatī’ti, hoti no tasmiṁ samaye anattamanatā hoti domanassaṁ ‘dūre no bhagavā bhavissatī’ti.
ne37:78.2	Yadā pana mayaṁ bhante bhagavantaṁ suṇoma ‘sāvatthiyā kosalesu cārikaṁ pakkanto’ti, hoti no tasmiṁ samaye anattamanatā hoti domanassaṁ ‘dūre no bhagavā’ti …pe….
ne37:79.1	Yadā pana mayaṁ, bhante, bhagavantaṁ suṇoma ‘kāsīsu magadhesu cārikaṁ pakkamissatī’ti, hoti no tasmiṁ samaye anattamanatā hoti domanassaṁ ‘dūre no bhagavā bhavissatī’ti.
ne37:79.2	Yadā pana mayaṁ, bhante, bhagavantaṁ suṇoma ‘kāsīsu magadhesu cārikaṁ pakkanto’ti, anappakā no tasmiṁ samaye anattamanatā hoti anappakaṁ domanassaṁ ‘dūre no bhagavā’ti.
ne37:80.1	Yadā pana mayaṁ, bhante, bhagavantaṁ suṇoma ‘magadhesu kāsīsu cārikaṁ pakkamissatī’ti, hoti no tasmiṁ samaye attamanatā hoti somanassaṁ ‘āsanne no bhagavā bhavissatī’ti.
ne37:80.2	Yadā pana mayaṁ bhante bhagavantaṁ suṇoma ‘magadhesu kāsīsu cārikaṁ pakkanto’ti, hoti no tasmiṁ samaye attamanatā hoti somanassaṁ ‘āsanne no bhagavā’ti …pe….
ne37:81.1	Yadā pana mayaṁ, bhante, bhagavantaṁ suṇoma ‘kosalesu sāvatthiṁ cārikaṁ pakkamissatī’ti.
ne37:81.2	Hoti no tasmiṁ samaye attamanatā hoti somanassaṁ ‘āsanne no bhagavā bhavissatī’ti.
ne37:82.1	Yadā pana mayaṁ, bhante, bhagavantaṁ suṇoma ‘sāvatthiyaṁ viharati jetavane anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme’ti hoti anappakā no tasmiṁ samaye attamanatā, hoti anappakaṁ somanassaṁ ‘āsanne no bhagavā’”ti.
ne37:83.1	“Tasmātiha, thapatayo, sambādho gharāvāso rajāpatho, abbhokāso pabbajjā, alañca pana vo, thapatayo, appamādāyā”ti.
ne37:83.2	“Atthi kho no, bhante, etamhā sambādhā añño sambādho sambādhataro ceva sambādhasaṅkhātataro cā”ti?
ne37:83.3	“Katamo pana vo, thapatayo, etamhā sambādhā añño sambādho sambādhataro ceva sambādhasaṅkhātataro cā”ti?
ne37:84.1	“Idha mayaṁ, bhante, yadā rājā pasenadi kosalo uyyānabhūmiṁ niyyātukāmo hoti, ye te rañño pasenadissa kosalassa nāgā opavayhā, te kappetvā yā tā rañño pasenadissa kosalassa pajāpatiyo piyā manāpā, tā ekaṁ purato ekaṁ pacchato nisīdāpema, tāsaṁ kho pana, bhante, bhaginīnaṁ evarūpo gandho hoti.
ne37:84.2	Seyyathāpi nāma gandhakaraṇḍakassa tāvadeva vivariyamānassa, yathā taṁ rājakaññānaṁ gandhena vibhūsitānaṁ.
ne37:84.3	Tāsaṁ kho pana, bhante, bhaginīnaṁ evarūpo kāyasamphasso hoti, seyyathāpi nāma tūlapicuno vā kappāsapicuno vā, yathā taṁ rājakaññānaṁ sukhedhitānaṁ.
ne37:84.4	Tasmiṁ kho pana, bhante, samaye nāgopi rakkhitabbo hoti.
ne37:84.5	Tāpi bhaginiyo rakkhitabbā honti.
ne37:84.6	Attāpi rakkhitabbo hoti.
ne37:84.7	Na kho pana mayaṁ, bhante, abhijānāma tāsu bhaginīsu pāpakaṁ cittaṁ uppādentā, ayaṁ kho no, bhante, etamhā sambādhā añño sambādho sambādhataro ceva sambādhasaṅkhātataro cā”ti.
ne37:85.1	“Tasmātiha, thapatayo, sambādho gharāvāso rajāpatho, abbhokāso pabbajjā.

ne37:85.3	Catūhi kho, thapatayo, dhammehi samannāgato ariyasāvako sotāpanno hoti avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyaṇo.
ne37:86.1	Katamehi catūhi?
ne37:86.2	Idha, thapatayo, sutavā ariyasāvako buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti itipi so bhagavā arahaṁ …pe… buddho bhagavāti, dhamme …pe…
ne37:86.3	saṅghe …pe… vigatamalamaccherena cetasā agāraṁ ajjhāvasati, muttacāgo payatapāṇi vossaggarato yācayogo dānasaṁvibhāgarato appaṭivibhattaṁ.
ne37:86.4	Imehi kho, thapatayo, catūhi dhammehi samannāgato ariyasāvako sotāpanno hoti avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyaṇo.

ne37:87.2	saṅghe …pe…
ne37:87.3	yaṁ kho pana kiñci kule deyyadhammaṁ, sabbaṁ taṁ appaṭivibhattaṁ sīlavantehi kalyāṇadhammehi, taṁ kiṁ maññatha, thapatayo, katividhā te kosalesu manussā ye tumhākaṁ samasamā yadidaṁ dānasaṁvibhāgehī”ti?

ne37:165.1	“Sīlavato, ānanda, na cetanā karaṇīyā ‘kinti me avippaṭisāro jāyeyyā’ti.
ne37:165.2	Dhammatā esā, ānanda, yaṁ sīlavato avippaṭisāro jāyeyya.
ne37:165.3	Avippaṭisārinā, ānanda, na cetanā karaṇīyā ‘kinti me pāmojjaṁ jāyeyyā’ti.
ne37:165.4	Dhammatā esā, ānanda, yaṁ avippaṭisārino pāmojjaṁ jāyeyya.
ne37:165.5	Pamuditena, ānanda, na cetanā karaṇīyā ‘kinti me pīti jāyeyyā’ti.
ne37:165.6	Dhammatā esā, ānanda, yaṁ pamuditassa pīti jāyeyya.
ne37:165.7	Pītimanassa, ānanda, na cetanā karaṇīyā ‘kinti me kāyo passambheyyā’ti.
ne37:165.8	Dhammatā esā, ānanda, yaṁ pītimanassa kāyo passambheyya.
ne37:165.9	Passaddhakāyassa, ānanda, na cetanā karaṇīyā ‘kintāhaṁ sukhaṁ vediyeyyan’ti.
ne37:165.10	Dhammatā esā, ānanda, yaṁ passaddhakāyo sukhaṁ vediyeyya.
ne37:165.11	Sukhino ānanda, na cetanā karaṇīyā ‘kinti me samādhi jāyeyyā’ti.
ne37:165.12	Dhammatā esā, ānanda, yaṁ sukhino samādhi jāyeyya.
ne37:165.13	Samāhitassa, ānanda, na cetanā karaṇīyā ‘kintāhaṁ yathābhūtaṁ pajāneyyan’ti.
ne37:165.14	Dhammatā esā, ānanda, yaṁ samāhito yathābhūtaṁ pajāneyya.
ne37:165.15	Yathābhūtaṁ pajānatā, ānanda, na cetanā karaṇīyā ‘kinti me nibbidā jāyeyyā’ti.
ne37:165.16	Dhammatā esā, ānanda, yaṁ yathābhūtaṁ pajānanto nibbindeyya.
ne37:165.17	Nibbindantena, ānanda, na cetanā karaṇīyā ‘kinti me virāgo jāyeyyā’ti.
ne37:165.18	Dhammatā esā, ānanda, yaṁ nibbindanto virajjeyya.
ne37:165.19	Virajjantena, ānanda, na cetanā karaṇīyā ‘kinti me vimutti jāyeyyā’ti.
ne37:165.20	Dhammatā esā, ānanda, yaṁ virajjanto vimucceyya.
ne37:165.21	Vimuttena, ānanda, na cetanā karaṇīyā ‘kinti me vimuttiñāṇadassanaṁ uppajjeyyā’ti.
ne37:165.22	Dhammatā esā, ānanda, yaṁ vimuttassa vimuttiñāṇadassanaṁ uppajjeyyā”ti.

ne37:262.1	Tatra kho, bhikkhave, ko viseso ko adhippayāso kiṁ nānākaraṇaṁ tathāgatassa arahato sammāsambuddhassa paññāvimuttena bhikkhunāti?
ne37:262.2	Bhagavaṁmūlakā no, bhante, dhammā …pe…
ne37:263.1	Tathāgato, bhikkhave, arahaṁ sammāsambuddho anuppannassa maggassa uppādetā, asañjātassa maggassa sañjanetā, anakkhātassa maggassa akkhātā, maggaññū maggavidū maggakovido, maggānugā ca, bhikkhave, etarahi sāvakā viharanti pacchā samannāgatā.
ne37:263.2	Ayaṁ kho, bhikkhave, viseso, ayaṁ adhippayāso, idaṁ nānākaraṇaṁ tathāgatassa arahato sammāsambuddhassa paññāvimuttena bhikkhunā”ti.
ne37:264.1	Idaṁ asekkhabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ.
ne37:265.1	Tattha katamaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyañca vāsanābhāgiyañca suttaṁ?
ne37:266.1	“Channamativassati,
ne37:266.2	vivaṭaṁ nātivassati;
ne37:266.3	Tasmā channaṁ vivaretha,
ne37:266.4	evaṁ taṁ nātivassatī”ti.
ne37:267.1	“Channamativassatī”ti saṅkileso, “vivaṭaṁ nātivassatī”ti vāsanā, “tasmā channaṁ vivaretha, evaṁ taṁ nātivassatī”ti ayaṁ saṅkileso ca vāsanā ca.
ne37:267.2	Idaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyañca vāsanābhāgiyañca suttaṁ.
ne37:268.1	“Cattārome, mahārāja, puggalā santo saṁvijjamānā lokasmiṁ.
ne37:268.2	Katame cattāro?
ne37:268.3	Tamo tamaparāyaṇo tamo jotiparāyaṇo joti tamaparāyaṇo joti jotiparāyaṇo”ti.
ne37:268.4	Tattha yo ca puggalo joti tamaparāyaṇo yo ca puggalo tamo tamaparāyaṇo, ime dve puggalā saṅkilesabhāgiyā, yo ca puggalo tamo jotiparāyaṇo yo ca puggalo joti jotiparāyaṇo, ime dve puggalā vāsanābhāgiyā.
ne37:268.5	Idaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyañca vāsanābhāgiyañca suttaṁ.
ne37:269.1	Tattha katamaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyañca nibbedhabhāgiyañca suttaṁ?
ne37:270.1	“Na taṁ daḷhaṁ bandhanamāhu dhīrā,
ne37:270.2	Yadāyasaṁ dārujapabbajañca;
ne37:270.3	Sārattarattā maṇikuṇḍalesu,
ne37:270.4	Puttesu dāresu ca yā apekkhā”ti.
ne37:271.1	Ayaṁ saṅkileso.
ne37:272.1	“Etaṁ daḷhaṁ bandhanamāhu dhīrā,
ne37:272.2	Ohārinaṁ sithilaṁ duppamuñcaṁ;
ne37:272.3	Etampi chetvāna paribbajanti,
ne37:272.4	Anapekkhino kāmasukhaṁ pahāyā”ti.
ne37:273.1	Ayaṁ nibbedho.
ne37:273.2	Idaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyañca nibbedhabhāgiyañca suttaṁ.
ne37:274.1	“Yañca, bhikkhave, ceteti, yañca pakappeti, yañca anuseti.
ne37:274.2	Ārammaṇametaṁ hoti viññāṇassa ṭhitiyā, ārammaṇe sati patiṭṭhā viññāṇassa hoti, tasmiṁ patiṭṭhite viññāṇe virūḷhe āyatiṁ punabbhavābhinibbatti hoti, āyatiṁ punabbhavābhinibbattiyā sati āyatiṁ jātijarāmaraṇaṁ sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsā sambhavanti, evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti.
ne37:275.1	No ce, bhikkhave, ceteti, no ce pakappeti, atha ce anuseti.
ne37:275.2	Ārammaṇametaṁ hoti viññāṇassa ṭhitiyā, ārammaṇe sati patiṭṭhā viññāṇassa hoti, tasmiṁ patiṭṭhite viññāṇe virūḷhe āyatiṁ punabbhavābhinibbatti hoti, āyatiṁ punabbhavābhinibbattiyā sati āyatiṁ jātijarāmaraṇaṁ sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsā sambhavanti, evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī”ti.
ne37:275.3	Ayaṁ saṅkileso.
ne37:276.1	“Yato ca kho, bhikkhave, no ceva ceteti, no ca pakappeti, no ca anuseti.
ne37:276.2	Ārammaṇametaṁ na hoti viññāṇassa ṭhitiyā, ārammaṇe asati patiṭṭhā viññāṇassa na hoti, tasmiṁ appatiṭṭhite viññāṇe avirūḷhe āyatiṁ punabbhavābhinibbatti na hoti, āyatiṁ punabbhavābhinibbattiyā asati āyatiṁ jātijarāmaraṇaṁ sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti.
ne37:276.3	Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa nirodho hotī”ti, ayaṁ nibbedho.
ne37:276.4	Idaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyañca nibbedhabhāgiyañca suttaṁ.
ne37:277.1	Tattha katamaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyañca asekkhabhāgiyañca suttaṁ?
ne37:278.1	“‘Samuddo samuddo’ti kho, bhikkhave, assutavā puthujjano bhāsati.
ne37:278.2	Neso, bhikkhave, ariyassa vinaye samuddo.
ne37:278.3	Mahā eso, bhikkhave, udakarāsi mahāudakaṇṇavo.
ne37:278.4	Cakkhu, bhikkhave, purisassa samuddo, tassa rūpamayo vego”ti.
ne37:278.5	Ayaṁ saṅkileso.
ne37:279.1	“Yo taṁ rūpamayaṁ vegaṁ sahati ayaṁ vuccati, bhikkhave, atari cakkhusamuddaṁ saūmiṁ sāvaṭṭaṁ sagahaṁ sarakkhasaṁ tiṇṇo pāraṅgato thale tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇo”ti.
ne37:279.2	Ayaṁ asekkho.
ne37:280.1	“Sotaṁ, bhikkhave …pe…
ne37:280.2	ghānaṁ …
ne37:280.3	jivhā …
ne37:280.4	kāyo …
ne37:280.5	mano, bhikkhave, purisassa samuddo tassa dhammamayo vego”ti.
ne37:280.6	Ayaṁ saṅkileso.
ne37:281.1	“Yo taṁ dhammamayaṁ vegaṁ sahati, ayaṁ vuccati, bhikkhave, atari manosamuddaṁ saūmiṁ sāvaṭṭaṁ sagahaṁ sarakkhasaṁ tiṇṇo pāraṅgato thale tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇo”ti.
ne37:281.2	Ayaṁ asekkho.
ne37:281.3	Idamavoca bhagavā.
ne37:281.4	Idaṁ vatvāna sugato athāparaṁ etadavoca satthā—
ne37:282.1	“Yo imaṁ samuddaṁ sagahaṁ sarakkhasaṁ,
ne37:282.2	Saūmiṁ sāvaṭṭaṁ sabhayaṁ duttaraṁ accatari;
ne37:282.3	Sa vedantagū vusitabrahmacariyo,
ne37:282.4	Lokantagū pāragatoti vuccatī”ti.
ne37:283.1	Ayaṁ asekkho.
ne37:283.2	Idaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyañca asekkhabhāgiyañca suttaṁ.
ne37:284.1	“Chayime, bhikkhave, baḷisā lokasmiṁ anayāya sattānaṁ byābādhāya pāṇīnaṁ.
ne37:284.2	Katame cha?
ne37:284.3	Santi, bhikkhave, cakkhuviññeyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṁhitā rajanīyā, tañce bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhosāya tiṭṭhati, ayaṁ vuccati, bhikkhave, bhikkhu gilitabaḷiso mārassa anayaṁ āpanno, byasanaṁ āpanno, yathākāmakaraṇīyo pāpimato.
ne37:285.1	Santi, bhikkhave, sotaviññeyyā saddā …pe…
ne37:285.2	ghānaviññeyyā gandhā …
ne37:285.3	jivhāviññeyyā rasā …
ne37:285.4	kāyaviññeyyā phoṭṭhabbā …
ne37:285.5	manoviññeyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṁhitā rajanīyā, tañce bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhosāya tiṭṭhati.
ne37:285.6	Ayaṁ vuccati, bhikkhave, bhikkhu gilitabaḷiso mārassa anayaṁ āpanno, byasanaṁ āpanno, yathākāmakaraṇīyo pāpimato”ti.
ne37:285.7	Ayaṁ saṅkileso.
ne37:286.1	Santi ca, bhikkhave, cakkhuviññeyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṁhitā rajanīyā, tañce bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhosāya tiṭṭhati, ayaṁ vuccati, bhikkhave, bhikkhu na gilitabaḷiso mārassa, abhedi baḷisaṁ, paribhedi baḷisaṁ, na anayaṁ āpanno, na byasanaṁ āpanno, na yathākāmakaraṇīyo pāpimato.
ne37:287.1	Santi ca, bhikkhave, sotaviññeyyā saddā …pe…
ne37:287.2	manoviññeyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṁhitā rajanīyā, tañce bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati, nājjhosāya tiṭṭhati.
ne37:287.3	Ayaṁ vuccati, bhikkhave, bhikkhu na gilitabaḷiso mārassa, abhedi baḷisaṁ, paribhedi baḷisaṁ, na anayaṁ āpanno, na byasanaṁ āpanno, na yathākāmakaraṇīyo pāpimato”ti.
ne37:287.4	Ayaṁ asekkho.
ne37:287.5	Idaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyañca asekkhabhāgiyañca suttaṁ.
ne37:288.1	Tattha katamaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyañca nibbedhabhāgiyañca asekkhabhāgiyañca suttaṁ?
ne37:289.1	“Ayaṁ loko santāpajāto,
ne37:289.2	Phassapareto rogaṁ vadati attato;
ne37:289.3	Yena yena hi maññanti,
ne37:289.4	Tato taṁ hoti aññathā.
ne37:290.1	Aññathābhāvī bhavasatto loko,
ne37:290.2	Bhavapareto bhavamevābhinandati;
ne37:290.3	Yadabhinandati taṁ bhayaṁ,
ne37:290.4	Yassa bhāyati taṁ dukkhan”ti.
ne37:290.5	Ayaṁ saṅkileso.
ne37:291.1	“Bhavavippahānāya kho panidaṁ brahmacariyaṁ vussatī”ti.
ne37:291.2	Ayaṁ nibbedho.
ne37:292.1	“Ye hi keci samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā bhavena bhavassa vippamokkhamāhaṁsu, sabbe te ‘avippamuttā bhavasmā’ti vadāmi.
ne37:292.2	Ye vā pana keci samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā vibhavena bhavassa nissaraṇamāhaṁsu, sabbe te ‘anissaṭā bhavasmā’ti vadāmi.
ne37:292.3	Upadhiṁ hi paṭicca dukkhamidaṁ sambhotī”ti.
ne37:292.4	Ayaṁ saṅkileso.
ne37:293.1	“Sabbupādānakkhayā natthi dukkhassa sambhavo”ti.
ne37:293.2	Ayaṁ nibbedho.
ne37:294.1	“Lokamimaṁ passa, puthū avijjāya paretā bhūtā bhūtaratā, bhavā aparimuttā, ye hi keci bhavā sabbadhi sabbatthatāya, sabbe te bhavā aniccā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā”ti.
ne37:294.2	Ayaṁ saṅkileso.
ne37:295.1	“Evametaṁ yathābhūtaṁ,
ne37:295.2	Sammappaññāya passato;
ne37:295.3	Bhavataṇhā pahīyati,
ne37:295.4	Vibhavaṁ nābhinandati;
ne37:295.5	Sabbaso taṇhānaṁ khayā,
ne37:295.6	Asesavirāganirodho nibbānan”ti.
ne37:296.1	Ayaṁ nibbedho.
ne37:297.1	“Tassa nibbutassa bhikkhuno,
ne37:297.2	Anupādā punabbhavo na hoti;
ne37:297.3	Abhibhūto māro vijitasaṅgāmo,
ne37:297.4	Upaccagā sabbabhavāni tādī”ti.
ne37:298.1	Ayaṁ asekkho.
ne37:298.2	Idaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyañca nibbedhabhāgiyañca asekkhabhāgiyañca suttaṁ.
ne37:299.1	“Cattārome, bhikkhave, puggalā.
ne37:299.2	Katame cattāro?
ne37:299.3	Anusotagāmī paṭisotagāmī ṭhitatto tiṇṇo pāraṅgato thale tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇo”ti.
ne37:299.4	Tattha yoyaṁ puggalo anusotagāmī, ayaṁ puggalo saṅkilesabhāgiyo.
ne37:299.5	Tattha yoyaṁ puggalo paṭisotagāmī yo ca ṭhitatto, ime dve puggalā nibbedhabhāgiyā.
ne37:299.6	Tattha yoyaṁ puggalo tiṇṇo pāraṅgato thale tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇo, ayaṁ asekkho.
ne37:299.7	Idaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyañca nibbedhabhāgiyañca asekkhabhāgiyañca suttaṁ.
ne37:300.1	Tattha katamaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyañca vāsanābhāgiyañca nibbedhabhāgiyañca suttaṁ?
ne37:301.1	Chaḷābhijātiko atthi puggalo kaṇho kaṇhābhijātiko kaṇhaṁ dhammaṁ abhijāyati, atthi puggalo kaṇho kaṇhābhijātiko sukkaṁ dhammaṁ abhijāyati, atthi puggalo kaṇho kaṇhābhijātiko akaṇhaṁ asukkaṁ akaṇhaasukkavipākaṁ accantadiṭṭhaṁ nibbānaṁ ārādheti, atthi puggalo sukko sukkābhijātiko kaṇhaṁ dhammaṁ abhijāyati, atthi puggalo sukko sukkābhijātiko sukkaṁ dhammaṁ abhijāyati, atthi puggalo sukko sukkābhijātiko akaṇhaṁ asukkaṁ akaṇhaasukkavipākaṁ accantadiṭṭhaṁ nibbānaṁ ārādheti.
ne37:302.1	Tattha yo ca puggalo kaṇho kaṇhābhijātiko kaṇhaṁ dhammaṁ abhijāyati, yo ca puggalo sukko sukkābhijātiko kaṇhaṁ dhammaṁ abhijāyati, ime dve puggalā saṅkilesabhāgiyā.
ne37:303.1	Tattha yo ca puggalo kaṇho kaṇhābhijātiko sukkaṁ dhammaṁ abhijāyati, yo ca puggalo sukko sukkābhijātiko sukkaṁ dhammaṁ abhijāyati, ime dve puggalā vāsanābhāgiyā.
ne37:304.1	Tattha yo ca puggalo kaṇho kaṇhābhijātiko akaṇhaṁ asukkaṁ akaṇhaasukkavipākaṁ accantadiṭṭhaṁ nibbānaṁ ārādheti, yo ca puggalo sukko sukkābhijātiko akaṇhaṁ asukkaṁ akaṇhaasukkavipākaṁ accantadiṭṭhaṁ nibbānaṁ ārādheti, ime dve puggalā nibbedhabhāgiyā, idaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyañca vāsanābhāgiyañca nibbedhabhāgiyañca suttaṁ.
ne37:305.1	“Cattārimāni, bhikkhave, kammāni.
ne37:305.2	Katamāni cattāri?
ne37:305.3	Atthi kammaṁ kaṇhaṁ kaṇhavipākaṁ, atthi kammaṁ sukkaṁ sukkavipākaṁ, atthi kammaṁ kaṇhasukkaṁ kaṇhasukkavipākaṁ, atthi kammaṁ akaṇhaṁ asukkaṁ akaṇhaasukkavipākaṁ kammuttamaṁ kammaseṭṭhaṁ kammakkhayāya saṁvattati”.
ne37:306.1	Tattha yañca kammaṁ kaṇhaṁ kaṇhavipākaṁ, yañca kammaṁ kaṇhasukkaṁ kaṇhasukkavipākaṁ, ayaṁ saṅkileso.
ne37:306.2	Yañca kammaṁ sukkaṁ sukkavipākaṁ, ayaṁ vāsanā.
ne37:306.3	Yañca kammaṁ akaṇhaṁ asukkaṁ akaṇhaasukkavipākaṁ kammuttamaṁ kammaseṭṭhaṁ kammakkhayāya saṁvattati, ayaṁ nibbedho.
ne37:306.4	Idaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyañca vāsanābhāgiyañca nibbedhabhāgiyañca suttaṁ.
ne37:307.1	Tattha katamaṁ vāsanābhāgiyañca, nibbedhabhāgiyañca suttaṁ?
ne37:308.1	“Laddhāna mānusattaṁ dve,
ne37:308.2	Kiccaṁ akiccameva ca;
ne37:308.3	Sukiccaṁ ceva puññāni,
ne37:308.4	Saṁyojanavippahānaṁ vā”ti.
ne37:309.1	“Sukiccaṁ ceva puññānī”ti vāsanā.
ne37:309.2	“Saṁyojanavippahānaṁ vā”ti nibbedho.
ne37:310.1	“Puññāni karitvāna,
ne37:310.2	Saggā saggaṁ vajanti katapuññā;
ne37:310.3	Saṁyojanappahānā,
ne37:310.4	Jarāmaraṇā vippamuccantī”ti.
ne37:311.1	“Puññāni karitvāna, saggā saggaṁ vajanti katapuññā”ti vāsanā.
ne37:311.2	“Saṁyojanappahānā jarāmaraṇā vippamuccantī”ti nibbedho.
ne37:311.3	Idaṁ vāsanābhāgiyañca nibbedhabhāgiyañca suttaṁ.
ne37:312.1	“Dvemāni, bhikkhave, padhānāni.
ne37:312.2	Katamāni dve?
ne37:312.3	Yo ca agārasmā anagāriyaṁ pabbajitesu cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhāraṁ pariccajati, yo ca agārasmā anagāriyaṁ pabbajitesu sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo taṇhakkhayo virāgo nirodho nibbānan”ti.
ne37:312.4	Tattha yo agārasmā anagāriyaṁ pabbajitesu cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhāraṁ pariccajati, ayaṁ vāsanā.
ne37:313.1	Yo agārasmā anagāriyaṁ pabbajitesu sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo taṇhakkhayo virāgo nirodho nibbānaṁ, ayaṁ nibbedho.
ne37:313.2	Idaṁ vāsanābhāgiyañca nibbedhabhāgiyañca suttaṁ.
ne37:314.1	Tattha taṇhāsaṅkilesabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ taṇhāpakkheneva niddisitabbaṁ tīhi taṇhāhi—
ne37:314.2	kāmataṇhāya bhavataṇhāya vibhavataṇhāya.
ne37:314.3	Yena yena vā pana vatthunā ajjhositā, tena teneva niddisitabbaṁ, tassā vitthāro chattiṁsataṇhājāliniyā vicaritāni.
ne37:315.1	Tattha diṭṭhisaṅkilesabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ diṭṭhipakkheneva niddisitabbaṁ ucchedasassatena, yena yena vā pana vatthunā diṭṭhivasena abhinivisati “idameva saccaṁ moghamaññan”ti, tena teneva niddisitabbaṁ, tassā vitthāro dvāsaṭṭhidiṭṭhigatāni.
ne37:316.1	Tattha duccaritasaṅkilesabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ cetanāya cetasikakammena niddisitabbaṁ tīhi duccaritehi—
ne37:316.2	kāyaduccaritena vacīduccaritena manoduccaritena, tassa vitthāro dasaakusalakammapathā.
ne37:317.1	Tattha taṇhāvodānabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ samathena niddisitabbaṁ, diṭṭhivodānabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ vipassanāya niddisitabbaṁ, duccaritavodānabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ sucaritena niddisitabbaṁ—
ne37:317.2	tīṇi akusalamūlāni.
ne37:317.3	Taṁ kissa hetu?
ne37:317.4	Saṁsārassa nibbattiyā.
ne37:317.5	Tathā nibbatte saṁsāre kāyaduccaritaṁ kāyasucaritaṁ vacīduccaritaṁ vacīsucaritaṁ manoduccaritaṁ manosucaritaṁ iminā asubhena kammavipākena idaṁ bālalakkhaṇaṁ nibbattatīti.
ne37:317.6	Idaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ.
ne37:318.1	Iminā subhena kammavipākena idaṁ mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṁ nibbattatīti.
ne37:318.2	Idaṁ vāsanābhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ.
ne37:319.1	Tattha saṅkilesabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ catūhi kilesabhūmīhi niddisitabbaṁ—
ne37:319.2	anusayabhūmiyā pariyuṭṭhānabhūmiyā saṁyojanabhūmiyā upādānabhūmiyā.
ne37:319.3	Sānusayassa pariyuṭṭhānaṁ jāyati, pariyuṭṭhito saṁyujjati, saṁyujjanto upādiyati, upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṁ sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsā sambhavanti, evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti.
ne37:319.4	Imāhi catūhi kilesabhūmīhi sabbe kilesā saṅgahaṁ samosaraṇaṁ gacchanti, idaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ.
ne37:320.1	Vāsanābhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ tīhi sucaritehi niddisitabbaṁ, nibbedhabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ catūhi saccehi niddisitabbaṁ, asekkhabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ tīhi dhammehi niddisitabbaṁ—
ne37:320.2	buddhadhammehi paccekabuddhadhammehi sāvakabhūmiyā.
ne37:320.3	Jhāyivisaye niddisitabbanti.

ne37:333.1	Yaṁ iha “saṁyojanavippahānaṁ vā”ti ca “saṁyojanappahānā, jarāmaraṇā vippamuccantī”ti ca, idaṁ lokuttaraṁ.
ne37:333.2	Idaṁ lokiyañca lokuttarañca.
ne37:334.1	“Viññāṇe ce, bhikkhave, āhāre sati nāmarūpassa avakkanti hoti, nāmarūpassa avakkantiyā sati punabbhavo hoti, punabbhave sati jāti hoti, jātiyā sati jarāmaraṇaṁ sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsā sambhavanti.
ne37:334.2	Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti.
ne37:334.3	Seyyathāpi, bhikkhave, mahārukkho, tassa yāni ceva mūlāni adhogamāni yāni ca tiriyaṅgamāni, sabbāni tāni uddhaṁ ojaṁ abhiharanti.
ne37:334.4	Evaṁ hi so, bhikkhave, mahārukkho tadāhāro tadupādāno ciraṁ dīghamaddhānaṁ tiṭṭheyya.
ne37:334.5	Evameva kho, bhikkhave, viññāṇe āhāre sati nāmarūpassa avakkanti hoti sabbaṁ …pe…
ne37:334.6	evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī”ti.
ne37:334.7	Idaṁ lokiyaṁ.
ne37:335.1	“Viññāṇe ce, bhikkhave, āhāre asati nāmarūpassa avakkanti na hoti, nāmarūpassa avakkantiyā asati punabbhavo na hoti, punabbhave asati jāti na hoti, jātiyā asati jarāmaraṇaṁ sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti.
ne37:335.2	Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa nirodho hoti.
ne37:335.3	Seyyathāpi, bhikkhave, mahārukkho atha puriso āgaccheyya kuddālapiṭakaṁ ādāya, so taṁ rukkhaṁ mūle chindeyya, mūle chetvā palikhaṇeyya, palikhaṇitvā mūlāni uddhareyya antamaso usīranāḷimattānipi.
ne37:335.4	So taṁ rukkhaṁ khaṇḍākhaṇḍikaṁ chindeyya, khaṇḍākhaṇḍikaṁ chinditvā phāleyya, phāletvā sakalikaṁ sakalikaṁ kareyya, sakalikaṁ sakalikaṁ karitvā vātātape visoseyya, vātātape visosetvā agginā ḍaheyya, agginā ḍahetvā masiṁ kareyya, masiṁ karitvā mahāvāte vā ophuṇeyya, nadiyā vā sīghasotāya pavāheyya, evaṁ hi so, bhikkhave, mahārukkho ucchinnamūlo assa tālāvatthukato anabhāvaṅkato āyatiṁ anuppādadhammo.
ne37:335.5	Evameva kho, bhikkhave, viññāṇe āhāre asati nāmarūpassa avakkanti na hoti, nāmarūpassa avakkantiyā asati sabbaṁ …pe…
ne37:335.6	evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa nirodho hotī”ti.

ne37:449.1	Idaṁ kammaṁ.
ne37:450.1	“Puna caparaṁ, bhikkhave, bālaṁ pīṭhasamārūḷhaṁ vā mañcasamārūḷhaṁ vā chamāyaṁ vā semānaṁ yānissa pubbe pāpakāni kammāni katāni kāyena duccaritāni vācāya duccaritāni manasā duccaritāni, tānissa tamhi samaye olambanti ajjholambanti abhippalambanti.
ne37:450.2	Seyyathāpi, bhikkhave, mahataṁ pabbatakūṭānaṁ chāyā sāyanhasamayaṁ pathaviyaṁ olambanti ajjholambanti abhippalambanti.
ne37:450.3	Evameva kho, bhikkhave, bālaṁ pīṭhasamārūḷhaṁ vā mañcasamārūḷhaṁ vā chamāyaṁ vā semānaṁ yānissa pubbe pāpakāni kammāni katāni kāyena duccaritāni vācāya duccaritāni manasā duccaritāni, tānissa tamhi samaye olambanti ajjholambanti abhippalambanti.
ne37:450.4	Tatra, bhikkhave, bālassa evaṁ hoti ‘akataṁ vata me kalyāṇaṁ, akataṁ kusalaṁ, akataṁ bhīruttāṇaṁ.
ne37:450.5	Kataṁ pāpaṁ, kataṁ luddaṁ, kataṁ kibbisaṁ, yāvatā bho akatakalyāṇānaṁ akatakusalānaṁ akatabhīruttāṇānaṁ katapāpānaṁ kataluddānaṁ katakibbisānaṁ gati, taṁ gatiṁ pecca gacchāmī’ti, so socati kilamati paridevati urattāḷiṁ kandati sammohaṁ āpajjatīti.
ne37:451.1	Puna caparaṁ, bhikkhave, paṇḍitaṁ pīṭhasamārūḷhaṁ vā mañcasamārūḷhaṁ vā chamāyaṁ vā semānaṁ yānissa pubbe kalyāṇāni kammāni katāni kāyena sucaritāni vācāya sucaritāni manasā sucaritāni, tānissa tamhi samaye olambanti ajjholambanti abhippalambanti.
ne37:451.2	Seyyathāpi, bhikkhave, mahataṁ pabbatakūṭānaṁ chāyā sāyanhasamayaṁ pathaviyaṁ olambanti ajjholambanti abhippalambanti.
ne37:451.3	Evameva kho, bhikkhave, paṇḍitaṁ pīṭhasamārūḷhaṁ vā mañcasamārūḷhaṁ vā chamāyaṁ vā semānaṁ yānissa pubbe kalyāṇāni kammāni katāni kāyena sucaritāni vācāya sucaritāni manasā sucaritāni, tānissa tamhi samaye olambanti ajjholambanti abhippalambanti.
ne37:451.4	Tatra, bhikkhave, paṇḍitassa ‘evaṁ hoti akataṁ vata me pāpaṁ, akataṁ luddaṁ, akataṁ kibbisaṁ.
ne37:451.5	Kataṁ kalyāṇaṁ, kataṁ kusalaṁ, kataṁ bhīruttāṇaṁ, yāvatā bho akatapāpānaṁ akataluddānaṁ akatakibbisānaṁ katakalyāṇānaṁ katakusalānaṁ katabhīruttāṇānaṁ gati, taṁ gatiṁ pecca gacchāmī’ti, so na socati na kilamati na paridevati na urattāḷiṁ kandati na sammohaṁ āpajjati, ‘kataṁ me puññaṁ, akataṁ pāpaṁ, yā bhavissati gati akatapāpassa akataluddassa akatakibbisassa katapuññassa katakusalassa katabhīruttāṇassa, taṁ pecca bhave gatiṁ paccanubhavissāmī’ti vippaṭisāro na jāyati.
ne37:451.6	Avippaṭisārino kho, bhikkhave, itthiyā vā purisassa vā gihino vā pabbajitassa vā bhaddakaṁ maraṇaṁ bhaddikā kālaṅkiriyāti vadāmī”ti.

ne37:504.3	Tattha yaṁ jaññā kāyasamācāraṁ “imaṁ kho me kāyasamācāraṁ sevato akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī”ti, evarūpo kāyasamācāro sevitabbo.

ne37:505.2	pariyesanampāhaṁ, devānaminda, duvidhena vadāmi sevitabbampi asevitabbampī”ti iti kho panetaṁ vuttaṁ, kiñcetaṁ paṭicca vuttaṁ.
ne37:505.3	Yathārūpañca kho pariyesanaṁ sevato akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, evarūpā pariyesanā na sevitabbā.
ne37:505.4	Tattha yaṁ jaññā pariyesanaṁ “imaṁ kho me pariyesanaṁ sevato akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī”ti, evarūpā pariyesanā sevitabbā.
ne37:506.1	“Pariyesanampāhaṁ, devānaminda, duvidhena vadāmi sevitabbampi asevitabbampī”ti iti yaṁ taṁ vuttaṁ, idametaṁ paṭicca vuttaṁ.
ne37:507.1	Tattha yaṁ āha—
ne37:507.2	“sevitabbampī”ti, idaṁ anuññātaṁ.
ne37:507.3	Yaṁ āha—
ne37:507.4	“na sevitabbampī”ti, idaṁ paṭikkhittaṁ.
ne37:507.5	Idaṁ anuññātañca paṭikkhittañca.
ne37:508.1	Tattha katamo thavo?
ne37:509.1	“Maggānaṭṭhaṅgiko seṭṭho,
ne37:509.2	saccānaṁ caturo padā;
ne37:509.3	Virāgo seṭṭho dhammānaṁ,
ne37:509.4	dvipadānañca cakkhumā”ti.
ne37:510.1	Ayaṁ thavo.
ne37:511.1	“Tīṇimāni, bhikkhave, aggāni.
ne37:511.2	Katamāni tīṇi?
ne37:511.3	Yāvatā, bhikkhave, sattā apadā vā dvipadā vā catuppadā vā bahuppadā vā rūpino vā arūpino vā saññino vā asaññino vā nevasaññīnāsaññino vā, tathāgato tesaṁ aggamakkhāyati seṭṭhamakkhāyati pavaramakkhāyati, yadidaṁ arahaṁ sammāsambuddho.
ne37:511.4	Yāvatā, bhikkhave, dhammānaṁ paṇṇattisaṅkhatānaṁ vā asaṅkhatānaṁ vā, virāgo tesaṁ dhammānaṁ aggamakkhāyati seṭṭhamakkhāyati pavaramakkhāyati, yadidaṁ madanimmadano …pe…
ne37:511.5	nirodho nibbānaṁ.
ne37:511.6	Yāvatā, bhikkhave, saṅghānaṁ paṇṇatti gaṇānaṁ paṇṇatti mahājanasannipātānaṁ paṇṇatti, tathāgatasāvakasaṅgho tesaṁ aggamakkhāyati seṭṭhamakkhāyati pavaramakkhāyati, yadidaṁ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā …pe…
ne37:511.7	puññakkhettaṁ lokassā”ti.
ne37:512.1	“Sabbalokuttaro satthā,
ne37:512.2	dhammo ca kusalakkhato;
ne37:512.3	Gaṇo ca narasīhassa,
ne37:512.4	tāni tīṇi vississare.
ne37:513.1	Samaṇapadumasañcayo gaṇo,
ne37:513.2	Dhammavaro ca vidūnaṁ sakkato;
ne37:513.3	Naravaradamako ca cakkhumā,
ne37:513.4	Tāni tīṇi lokassa uttari.
ne37:514.1	Satthā ca appaṭisamo,
ne37:514.2	Dhammo ca sabbo nirupadāho;
ne37:514.3	Ariyo ca gaṇavaro,
ne37:514.4	Tāni khalu vississare tīṇi.
ne37:515.1	Saccanāmo jino khemo sabbābhibhū,
ne37:515.2	Saccadhammo natthañño tassa uttari;
ne37:515.3	Ariyasaṅgho niccaṁ viññūnaṁ pūjito,
ne37:515.4	Tāni tīṇi lokassa uttari.
ne37:516.1	Ekāyanaṁ jātikhayantadassī,
ne37:516.2	Maggaṁ pajānāti hitānukampī;
ne37:516.3	Etena maggena tariṁsu pubbe,
ne37:516.4	Tarissanti ye ca taranti oghaṁ.
ne37:517.1	Taṁ tādisaṁ devamanussaseṭṭhaṁ,
ne37:517.2	Sattā namassanti visuddhipekkhā”ti.
ne37:518.1	Ayaṁ thavoti.
ne37:519.1	Tattha lokiyaṁ suttaṁ dvīhi suttehi niddisitabbaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyena ca vāsanābhāgiyena ca.
ne37:519.2	Lokuttarampi suttaṁ tīhi suttehi niddisitabbaṁ dassanabhāgiyena ca bhāvanābhāgiyena ca asekkhabhāgiyena ca.
ne37:519.3	Lokiyañca lokuttarañca.
ne37:519.4	Yasmiṁ sutte yaṁ yaṁ padaṁ dissati saṅkilesabhāgiyaṁ vā vāsanābhāgiyaṁ vā, tena tena lokiyanti niddisitabbaṁ, dassanabhāgiyaṁ vā bhāvanābhāgiyaṁ vā asekkhabhāgiyaṁ vā yaṁ yaṁ padaṁ dissati tena tena lokuttaranti niddisitabbaṁ.
ne37:520.1	Vāsanābhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyassa suttassa nigghātāya, dassanabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ vāsanābhāgiyassa suttassa nigghātāya, bhāvanābhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ dassanabhāgiyassa suttassa paṭinissaggāya, asekkhabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ bhāvanābhāgiyassa suttassa paṭinissaggāya, asekkhabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāratthaṁ.
ne37:521.1	Lokuttaraṁ suttaṁ sattādhiṭṭhānaṁ chabbīsatiyā puggalehi niddisitabbaṁ, te tīhi suttehi samanvesitabbā dassanabhāgiyena bhāvanābhāgiyena asekkhabhāgiyena cāti.
ne37:522.1	Tattha dassanabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ pañcahi puggalehi niddisitabbaṁ ekabījinā kolaṅkolena sattakkhattuparamena saddhānusārinā dhammānusārinā cāti, dassanabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ imehi pañcahi puggalehi niddisitabbaṁ.
ne37:522.2	Bhāvanābhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ dvādasahi puggalehi niddisitabbaṁ sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipannena, sakadāgāminā, anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipannena, anāgāminā, antarā parinibbāyinā, upahacca parinibbāyinā, asaṅkhāraparinibbāyinā, sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyinā, uddhaṁsotena akaniṭṭhagāminā, saddhāvimuttena, diṭṭhippattena, kāyasakkhinā cāti, bhāvanābhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ imehi dvādasahi puggalehi niddisitabbaṁ.
ne37:522.3	Asekkhabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ navahi puggalehi niddisitabbaṁ saddhāvimuttena, paññāvimuttena, suññatavimuttena, animittavimuttena, appaṇihitavimuttena, ubhatobhāgavimuttena samasīsinā paccekabuddhasammāsambuddhehi cāti, asekkhabhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ imehi navahi puggalehi niddisitabbaṁ.
ne37:522.4	Evaṁ lokuttaraṁ suttaṁ sattādhiṭṭhānaṁ imehi chabbīsatiyā puggalehi niddisitabbaṁ.
ne37:523.1	Lokiyaṁ suttaṁ sattādhiṭṭhānaṁ ekūnavīsatiyā puggalehi niddisitabbaṁ.
ne37:523.2	Te caritehi niddiṭṭhā samanvesitabbā keci rāgacaritā, keci dosacaritā, keci mohacaritā, keci rāgacaritā ca dosacaritā ca, keci rāgacaritā ca mohacaritā ca, keci dosacaritā ca mohacaritā ca, keci rāgacaritā ca dosacaritā ca mohacaritā ca, rāgamukhe ṭhito rāgacarito, rāgamukhe ṭhito dosacarito, rāgamukhe ṭhito mohacarito, rāgamukhe ṭhito rāgacarito ca dosacarito ca mohacarito ca, dosamukhe ṭhito dosacarito, dosamukhe ṭhito mohacarito, dosamukhe ṭhito rāgacarito, dosamukhe ṭhito rāgacarito ca dosacarito ca mohacarito ca, mohamukhe ṭhito mohacarito, mohamukhe ṭhito rāgacarito mohamukhe ṭhito dosacarito, mohamukhe ṭhito rāgacarito ca dosacarito ca mohacarito cāti, lokiyaṁ suttaṁ sattādhiṭṭhānaṁ imehi ekūnavīsatiyā puggalehi niddisitabbaṁ.
ne37:524.1	Vāsanābhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ sīlavantehi niddisitabbaṁ, te sīlavanto pañca puggalā pakatisīlaṁ samādānasīlaṁ cittappasādo samatho vipassanā cāti, vāsanābhāgiyaṁ suttaṁ imehi pañcahi puggalehi niddisitabbaṁ.
ne37:525.1	Lokuttaraṁ suttaṁ dhammādhiṭṭhānaṁ tīhi suttehi niddisitabbaṁ dassanabhāgiyena bhāvanābhāgiyena asekkhabhāgiyena ca.
ne37:526.1	Lokiyañca lokuttarañca sattādhiṭṭhānañca dhammādhiṭṭhānañca ubhayena niddisitabbaṁ, ñāṇaṁ paññāya niddisitabbaṁ paññindriyena paññābalena adhipaññāsikkhāya dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgena sammādiṭṭhiyā tīraṇāya santīraṇāya dhamme ñāṇena anvaye ñāṇena khaye ñāṇena anuppāde ñāṇena anaññātaññassāmītindriyena aññindriyena aññātāvindriyena cakkhunā vijjāya buddhiyā bhūriyā medhāya, yaṁ yaṁ vā pana labbhati, tena tena paññādhivacanena niddisitabbaṁ.
ne37:527.1	Ñeyyaṁ atītānāgatapaccuppannehi ajjhattikabāhirehi hīnappaṇītehi dūrasantikehi saṅkhatāsaṅkhatehi kusalākusalābyākatehi saṅkhepato vā chahi ārammaṇehi niddisitabbaṁ.
ne37:527.2	Ñāṇañca ñeyyañca tadubhayena niddisitabbaṁ, paññāpi ārammaṇabhūtā ñeyyaṁ, yaṁ kiñci ārammaṇabhūtaṁ ajjhattikaṁ vā bāhiraṁ vā, sabbaṁ taṁ saṅkhatena asaṅkhatena ca niddisitabbaṁ.
ne37:528.1	Dassanaṁ bhāvanā sakavacanaṁ paravacanaṁ visajjanīyaṁ avisajjanīyaṁ kammaṁ vipākoti sabbattha tadubhayaṁ sutte yathā niddiṭṭhaṁ, tathā upadhārayitvā labbhamānato niddisitabbaṁ, yaṁ vā pana kiñci bhagavā aññataravacanaṁ bhāsati, sabbaṁ taṁ yathāniddiṭṭhaṁ dhārayitabbaṁ.
ne37:529.1	Duvidho hetu yañca kammaṁ ye ca kilesā, samudayo kilesā.
ne37:529.2	Tattha kilesā saṅkilesabhāgiyena suttena niddisitabbā.
ne37:529.3	Samudayo saṅkilesabhāgiyena ca vāsanābhāgiyena ca suttena niddisitabbo.
ne37:529.4	Tattha kusalaṁ catūhi suttehi niddisitabbaṁ vāsanābhāgiyena dassanabhāgiyena bhāvanābhāgiyena asekkhabhāgiyena ca.
ne37:529.5	Akusalaṁ saṅkilesabhāgiyena suttena niddisitabbaṁ.
ne37:529.6	Kusalañca akusalañca tadubhayena niddisitabbaṁ.
ne37:529.7	Anuññātaṁ bhagavato anuññātāya niddisitabbaṁ, taṁ pañcavidhaṁ saṁvaro pahānaṁ bhāvanā sacchikiriyā kappiyānulomoti, yaṁ dissati tāsu tāsu bhūmīsu, taṁ kappiyānulomena niddisitabbaṁ.
ne37:529.8	Paṭikkhittaṁ bhagavatā paṭikkhittakāraṇena niddisitabbaṁ.
ne37:529.9	Anuññātañca paṭikkhittañca tadubhayena niddisitabbaṁ.
ne37:529.10	Thavo pasaṁsāya niddisitabbo.
ne37:529.11	So pañcavidhena veditabbo bhagavato dhammassa ariyasaṅghassa ariyadhammānaṁ sikkhāya lokiyaguṇasampattiyāti.
ne37:529.12	Evaṁ thavo pañcavidhena niddisitabbo.
ne37:530.1	Indriyabhūmi navahi padehi niddisitabbā, kilesabhūmi navahi padehi niddisitabbā, evametāni aṭṭhārasa padāni honti nava padāni kusalāni nava padāni akusalānīti, tathāhi vuttaṁ “aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā kuhiṁ daṭṭhabbā, sāsanappaṭṭhāne”ti.
ne37:530.2	Tenāha āyasmā mahākaccāyano—
ne37:531.1	“Navahi ca padehi kusalā,
ne37:531.2	Navahi ca yujjanti akusalapakkhā;
ne37:531.3	Ete kho mūlapadā,
ne37:531.4	Bhavanti aṭṭhārasa padānī”ti.
ne37:531.5	Niyuttaṁ sāsanappaṭṭhānaṁ.
ne37:531.6	Ettāvatā samattā netti yā āyasmatā mahākaccāyanena bhāsitā bhagavatā anumoditā mūlasaṅgītiyaṁ saṅgītāti.
